    33→	granularCommitPrompt := `Please do the following tasks, committing after each one:
    34→
    35→1. Create a file called file1.go with this content:
    36→   package main
    37→   func One() int { return 1 }
    38→   Then run: git add file1.go && git commit -m "Add file1"
    39→
    40→2. Create a file called file2.go with this content:
    41→   package main
    42→   func Two() int { return 2 }
    43→   Then run: git add file2.go && git commit -m "Add file2"
    44→
    45→3. Create a file called file3.go with this content:
    46→   package main
    47→   func Three() int { return 3 }
    48→   Then run: git add file3.go && git commit -m "Add file3"
    49→
    50→Do each task in order, making the commit after each file creation.`
    51→
    52→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(granularCommitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
    55→	t.Logf("Agent output: %s", result.Stdout)
    56→
    57→	// Verify all files were created
    58→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file1.go"), "file1.go should exist")
    59→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file2.go"), "file2.go should exist")
    60→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file3.go"), "file3.go should exist")
    61→
    62→	// Verify multiple commits were made
    63→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
    64→	t.Logf("Commits after: %d", commitsAfter)
    65→	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, commitsAfter-commitsBefore, 3, "Should have at least 3 new commits")
    66→
    67→	// Get all checkpoint IDs from history
    68→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
    69→	t.Logf("Found %d checkpoint IDs in commit history", len(checkpointIDs))
    70→	for i, id := range checkpointIDs {
    71→		t.Logf("  Checkpoint %d: %s", i, id)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID (1:1 model)
    75→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 3 {
    76→		// Verify checkpoint IDs are unique
    77→		idSet := make(map[string]bool)
    78→		for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
    79→			require.Falsef(t, idSet[id], "Checkpoint IDs should be unique: %s is duplicated", id)
    80→			idSet[id] = true
    81→		}
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Verify metadata branch exists
    85→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
    86→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
    87→
    88→	// Validate each checkpoint has proper metadata and content
    89→	// Note: checkpointIDs are in reverse chronological order (newest first)
    90→	// So checkpointIDs[0] = file3.go, [1] = file2.go, [2] = file1.go
    91→	//
    92→	// With deferred finalization, all checkpoints from the same turn get the
    93→	// FULL transcript at turn end, so all checkpoints should contain all file names.
    94→	allFiles := []string{"file1.go", "file2.go", "file3.go"}
    95→	for i, cpID := range checkpointIDs {
    96→		fileNum := len(checkpointIDs) - i // Reverse the index to match file numbers
    97→		fileName := fmt.Sprintf("file%d.go", fileNum)
    98→		t.Logf("Validating checkpoint %d: %s (files_touched: %s)", i, cpID, fileName)
    99→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   100→			CheckpointID:              cpID,
   101→			Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   102→			FilesTouched:              []string{fileName},
   103→			ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // All checkpoints have full transcript
   104→		})
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// No shadow branches should remain after agent committed all files
   108→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   109→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   110→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   111→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   112→	}
   113→}
   114→
   115→// TestE2E_Scenario4_UserSplitsCommits tests user splitting agent changes into multiple commits.
   116→//
   117→// Scenario 4: User Splits Changes Into Multiple Commits
   118→// - Agent makes changes to multiple files
   119→// - User commits only some files first
   120→// - Uncommitted files are carried forward
   121→// - User commits remaining files later
   122→// - Each commit gets its own checkpoint ID
   123→func TestE2E_Scenario4_UserSplitsCommits(t *testing.T) {
   124→	t.Parallel()
   125→
   126→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   127→
   128→	// Agent creates multiple files in one prompt
   129→	multiFilePrompt := `Create these files:
   130→1. fileA.go with content: package main; func A() string { return "A" }
   131→2. fileB.go with content: package main; func B() string { return "B" }
   132→3. fileC.go with content: package main; func C() string { return "C" }
   133→4. fileD.go with content: package main; func D() string { return "D" }
   134→
   135→Create all four files, no other files or actions.`
   136→
   137→	result, err := env.RunAgent(multiFilePrompt)
   138→	require.NoError(t, err)
   139→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   140→
   141→	// Verify all files were created
   142→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileA.go"), "fileA.go should exist")
   143→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileB.go"), "fileB.go should exist")
   144→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileC.go"), "fileC.go should exist")
   145→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileD.go"), "fileD.go should exist")
   146→
   147→	// Check rewind points before commit
   148→	pointsBefore := env.GetRewindPoints()
   149→	t.Logf("Rewind points before commit: %d", len(pointsBefore))
   150→
   151→	// User commits only A, B first
   152→	t.Log("Committing fileA.go and fileB.go only")
   153→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add files A and B", "fileA.go", "fileB.go")
   154→
   155→	// Verify first checkpoint was created
   156→	checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   157→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
   158→	// Note: GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory returns IDs in reverse chronological order (newest first)
   159→	checkpointAB := checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit[0]
   160→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for A,B commit: %s", checkpointAB)
   161→
   162→	// Check rewind points - should still have points for uncommitted files
   163→	pointsAfterFirst := env.GetRewindPoints()
   164→	t.Logf("Rewind points after first commit: %d", len(pointsAfterFirst))
   165→
   166→	// User commits C, D later
   167→	t.Log("Committing fileC.go and fileD.go")
   168→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add files C and D", "fileC.go", "fileD.go")
   169→
   170→	// Verify second checkpoint was created with different ID
   171→	checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   172→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
   173→	checkpointCD := checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit[0] // Most recent (C,D commit) is first
   174→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for C,D commit: %s", checkpointCD)
   175→
   176→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID (1:1 model)
   177→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointAB, checkpointCD,
   178→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
   179→
   180→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   181→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   182→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   183→
   184→	// Both checkpoints are from the same session where agent created all 4 files.
   185→	// The transcript should contain all file names since it's the same agent work.
   186→	allFiles := []string{"fileA.go", "fileB.go", "fileC.go", "fileD.go"}
   187→
   188→	// Validate first checkpoint (files A, B committed)
   189→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   190→		CheckpointID:              checkpointAB,
   191→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   192→		FilesTouched:              []string{"fileA.go", "fileB.go"},
   193→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // Full session transcript
   194→	})
   195→
   196→	// Validate second checkpoint (files C, D committed)
   197→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   198→		CheckpointID:              checkpointCD,
   199→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   200→		FilesTouched:              []string{"fileC.go", "fileD.go"},
   201→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // Full session transcript
   202→	})
   203→
   204→	// CRITICAL: After all carry-forward files are committed, no shadow branches
   205→	// should remain. Only entire/checkpoints/v1 should exist. Extra shadow
   206→	// branches (entire/<hash>-<hash>) indicate a regression in carry-forward cleanup.
   207→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   208→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   209→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   210→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   211→	}
   212→	t.Logf("Entire branches after all commits: %v", entireBranches)
   213→}
   214→
   215→// TestE2E_Scenario5_PartialCommitStashNextPrompt tests partial commit, stash, then new prompt.
   216→//
   217→// Scenario 5: Partial Commit → Stash → Next Prompt
   218→// - Agent makes changes to A, B, C
   219→// - User commits A only
   220→// - User stashes B, C
   221→// - User runs another prompt (creates D, E)
   222→// - User commits D, E
   223→// - FilesTouched accumulates across prompts
   224→func TestE2E_Scenario5_PartialCommitStashNextPrompt(t *testing.T) {
   225→	t.Parallel()
   226→
   227→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   228→
   229→	// Prompt 1: Agent creates files A, B, C
   230→	t.Log("Prompt 1: Creating files A, B, C")
   231→	prompt1 := `Create these files:
   232→1. stash_a.go with content: package main; func StashA() {}
   233→2. stash_b.go with content: package main; func StashB() {}
   234→3. stash_c.go with content: package main; func StashC() {}
   235→Create all three files, nothing else.`
   236→
   237→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt1)
   238→	require.NoError(t, err)
   239→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   240→
   241→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_a.go"))
   242→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_b.go"))
   243→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_c.go"))
   244→
   245→	// User commits A only
   246→	t.Log("Committing stash_a.go only")
   247→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add stash_a", "stash_a.go")
   248→
   249→	// User stashes B, C
   250→	t.Log("Stashing remaining files")
   251→	env.GitStash()
   252→
   253→	// Verify B, C are no longer in working directory
   254→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("stash_b.go"), "stash_b.go should be stashed")
   255→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("stash_c.go"), "stash_c.go should be stashed")
   256→
   257→	// Prompt 2: Agent creates files D, E
   258→	t.Log("Prompt 2: Creating files D, E")
   259→	prompt2 := `Create these files:
   260→1. stash_d.go with content: package main; func StashD() {}
   261→2. stash_e.go with content: package main; func StashE() {}
   262→Create both files, nothing else.`
   263→
   264→	result, err = env.RunAgent(prompt2)
   265→	require.NoError(t, err)
   266→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   267→
   268→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_d.go"))
   269→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_e.go"))
   270→
   271→	// User commits D, E
   272→	t.Log("Committing stash_d.go and stash_e.go")
   273→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add stash_d and stash_e", "stash_d.go", "stash_e.go")
   274→
   275→	// Verify checkpoint was created for D, E
   276→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   277→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 2, "Should have checkpoints for both commits")
   278→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   279→
   280→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   281→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   282→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   283→
   284→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata
   285→	// checkpointIDs[0] is the most recent (D, E commit from prompt 2)
   286→	// checkpointIDs[1] is the earlier commit (A only from prompt 1)
   287→	//
   288→	// Note: We don't validate transcript content here because:
   289→	// 1. Claude's response text varies and may not contain exact file names
   290→	// 2. Multi-session checkpoints have multiple session folders, making validation complex
   291→	// The key validation is that files_touched is correct for each checkpoint.
   292→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 2 {
   293→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   294→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[0],
   295→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   296→			FilesTouched: []string{"stash_d.go", "stash_e.go"},
   297→		})
   298→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   299→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[1],
   300→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   301→			FilesTouched: []string{"stash_a.go"},
   302→		})
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	// Note: stash_b.go and stash_c.go are still stashed (never committed),
   306→	// so a shadow branch may remain for the session that still has pending files.
   307→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   308→	t.Logf("Entire branches after commits: %v", entireBranches)
   309→}
   310→
   311→// TestE2E_Scenario6_StashSecondPromptUnstashCommitAll tests stash, new prompt, unstash, commit all.
   312→//
   313→// Scenario 6: Stash → Second Prompt → Unstash → Commit All
   314→// - Agent makes changes to A, B, C
   315→// - User commits A only
   316→// - User stashes B, C
   317→// - User runs another prompt (creates D, E)
   318→// - User unstashes B, C
   319→// - User commits ALL (B, C, D, E) together
   320→// - All files link to single checkpoint
   321→func TestE2E_Scenario6_StashSecondPromptUnstashCommitAll(t *testing.T) {
   322→	t.Parallel()
   323→
   324→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   325→
   326→	// Prompt 1: Agent creates files A, B, C
   327→	t.Log("Prompt 1: Creating files A, B, C")
   328→	prompt1 := `Create these files:
   329→1. combo_a.go with content: package main; func ComboA() {}
   330→2. combo_b.go with content: package main; func ComboB() {}
   331→3. combo_c.go with content: package main; func ComboC() {}
   332→Create all three files, nothing else.`
   333→
   334→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt1)
   335→	require.NoError(t, err)
   336→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   337→
   338→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_a.go"))
   339→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"))
   340→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"))
   341→
   342→	// User commits A only
   343→	t.Log("Committing combo_a.go only")
   344→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add combo_a", "combo_a.go")
   345→
   346→	// User stashes B, C
   347→	t.Log("Stashing remaining files B, C")
   348→	env.GitStash()
   349→
   350→	// Verify B, C are no longer in working directory
   351→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"), "combo_b.go should be stashed")
   352→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"), "combo_c.go should be stashed")
   353→
   354→	// Prompt 2: Agent creates files D, E
   355→	t.Log("Prompt 2: Creating files D, E")
   356→	prompt2 := `Create these files:
   357→1. combo_d.go with content: package main; func ComboD() {}
   358→2. combo_e.go with content: package main; func ComboE() {}
   359→Create both files, nothing else.`
   360→
   361→	result, err = env.RunAgent(prompt2)
   362→	require.NoError(t, err)
   363→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   364→
   365→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_d.go"))
   366→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_e.go"))
   367→
   368→	// User unstashes B, C
   369→	t.Log("Unstashing B, C")
   370→	env.GitStashPop()
   371→
   372→	// Verify B, C are back
   373→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"), "combo_b.go should be back after unstash")
   374→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"), "combo_c.go should be back after unstash")
   375→
   376→	// User commits ALL files together (B, C, D, E)
   377→	t.Log("Committing all remaining files together")
   378→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add combo_b, combo_c, combo_d, combo_e",
   379→		"combo_b.go", "combo_c.go", "combo_d.go", "combo_e.go")
   380→
   381→	// Verify checkpoint was created
   382→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   383→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 2, "Should have checkpoints")
   384→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   385→
   386→	// The second commit should have all 4 files linked to a single checkpoint
   387→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   388→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   389→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   390→
   391→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata
   392→	// checkpointIDs[0] is the most recent (B, C, D, E combined commit)
   393→	// checkpointIDs[1] is the earlier commit (A only)
   394→	//
   395→	// Prompt 1 created A, B, C. User committed A, then stashed B, C.
   396→	// Prompt 2 created D, E. User unstashed B, C, then committed all 4 together.
   397→	//
   398→	// Note: We don't validate transcript content here because:
   399→	// 1. Claude's response text varies and may not contain exact file names
   400→	// 2. Multi-session checkpoints have multiple session folders, making validation complex
   401→	// The key validation is that files_touched is correct for each checkpoint.
   402→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 2 {
   403→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   404→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[0],
   405→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   406→			FilesTouched: []string{"combo_b.go", "combo_c.go", "combo_d.go", "combo_e.go"},
   407→		})
   408→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   409→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[1],
   410→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   411→			FilesTouched: []string{"combo_a.go"},
   412→		})
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	// No shadow branches should remain after all files committed
   416→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   417→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   418→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   419→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   420→	}
   421→}
   422→
   423→// TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingWithGitAddP tests partial staging with git add -p.
   424→//
   425→// Scenario 7: Partial Staging with `git add -p`
   426→// - Agent makes multiple changes to a single file
   427→// - User stages only part of the changes (simulated with partial write)
   428→// - Content-aware carry-forward detects partial commit
   429→// - Remaining changes carried forward to next commit
   430→func TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingSimulated(t *testing.T) {
   431→	t.Parallel()
   432→
   433→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   434→
   435→	// Create partial.go as an existing tracked file first.
   436→	// For MODIFIED files (vs NEW files), content-aware detection always
   437→	// creates checkpoints regardless of content changes. This allows us
   438→	// to test the partial staging scenario.
   439→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", "package main\n\n// placeholder\n")
   440→	env.GitAdd("partial.go")
   441→	env.GitCommit("Add placeholder partial.go")
   442→
   443→	// Agent modifies the file with multiple functions
   444→	t.Log("Agent modifying file with multiple functions")
   445→	multiLinePrompt := `Replace the contents of partial.go with this exact content:
   446→package main
   447→
   448→func First() int {
   449→	return 1
   450→}
   451→
   452→func Second() int {
   453→	return 2
   454→}
   455→
   456→func Third() int {
   457→	return 3
   458→}
   459→
   460→func Fourth() int {
   461→	return 4
   462→}
   463→
   464→Replace the file with exactly this content, nothing else.`
   465→
   466→	result, err := env.RunAgent(multiLinePrompt)
   467→	require.NoError(t, err)
   468→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   469→
   470→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("partial.go"))
   471→
   472→	// Check rewind points before commit
   473→	pointsBefore := env.GetRewindPoints()
   474→	t.Logf("Rewind points before commit: %d", len(pointsBefore))
   475→
   476→	// Simulate partial staging by temporarily replacing file with partial content
   477→	// Save the full content
   478→	fullContent := env.ReadFile("partial.go")
   479→	t.Logf("Full content length: %d bytes", len(fullContent))
   480→
   481→	// Write partial content (only first two functions)
   482→	partialContent := `package main
   483→
   484→func First() int {
   485→	return 1
   486→}
   487→
   488→func Second() int {
   489→	return 2
   490→}
   491→`
   492→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", partialContent)
   493→
   494→	// Commit the partial content
   495→	t.Log("Committing partial content (First and Second functions)")
   496→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add first two functions", "partial.go")
   497→
   498→	// Verify first checkpoint was created
   499→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   500→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
   501→	t.Logf("First checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointIDs[0])
   502→
   503→	// Restore the full content (simulating remaining changes still in worktree)
   504→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", fullContent)
   505→
   506→	// Commit the remaining content
   507→	t.Log("Committing full content (all functions)")
   508→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add remaining functions", "partial.go")
   509→
   510→	// Verify second checkpoint was created
   511→	checkpointIDsAfter := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   512→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfter), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
   513→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDsAfter)
   514→
   515→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID
   516→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointIDsAfter[0], checkpointIDsAfter[1],
   517→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
   518→
   519→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata and transcripts
   520→	// checkpointIDsAfter[0] is the most recent (full content commit)
   521→	// checkpointIDsAfter[1] is the earlier commit (partial content)
   522→	//
   523→	// Both commits are from the same session (single prompt), so both have
   524→	// the same full transcript referencing partial.go and the function names.
   525→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   526→		CheckpointID:              checkpointIDsAfter[0],
   527→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   528→		FilesTouched:              []string{"partial.go"},
   529→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"partial.go", "First", "Second", "Third", "Fourth"},
   530→	})
   531→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   532→		CheckpointID:              checkpointIDsAfter[1],
   533→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   534→		FilesTouched:              []string{"partial.go"},
   535→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"partial.go", "First", "Second", "Third", "Fourth"},
   536→	})
   537→}
   538→
   539→// TestE2E_ContentAwareOverlap_RevertAndReplace tests content-aware overlap detection
   540→// when user reverts session changes and writes different content.
   541→//
   542→// Content-Aware Overlap Detection:
   543→// - Agent creates file X with content "hello"
   544→// - User reverts X (git checkout -- X)
   545→// - User writes completely different content
   546→// - User commits
   547→// - Content mismatch → NO checkpoint trailer added
   548→func TestE2E_ContentAwareOverlap_RevertAndReplace(t *testing.T) {
   549→	t.Parallel()
   550→
   551→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   552→
   553→	// Agent creates a file
   554→	t.Log("Agent creating file with specific content")
   555→	createPrompt := `Create a file called overlap_test.go with this exact content:
   556→package main
   557→
   558→func OverlapOriginal() string {
   559→	return "original content from agent"
   560→}
   561→
   562→Create only this file.`
   563→
   564→	result, err := env.RunAgent(createPrompt)
   565→	require.NoError(t, err)
   566→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   567→
   568→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("overlap_test.go"))
   569→	originalContent := env.ReadFile("overlap_test.go")
   570→	t.Logf("Original content: %s", originalContent)
   571→
   572→	// Verify rewind points exist (session tracked the change)
   573→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   574→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(points), 1, "Should have rewind points")
   575→
   576→	// User reverts the file and writes completely different content
   577→	t.Log("User reverting file and writing different content")
   578→	differentContent := `package main
   579→
   580→func CompletelyDifferent() string {
   581→	return "user wrote this, not the agent"
   582→}
   583→`
   584→	env.WriteFile("overlap_test.go", differentContent)
   585→
   586→	// Verify content is different
   587→	currentContent := env.ReadFile("overlap_test.go")
   588→	assert.NotEqual(t, originalContent, currentContent)
   589→
   590→	// Commits before this test
   591→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
   592→
   593→	// User commits the different content
   594→	t.Log("Committing user-written content")
   595→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add overlap test file", "overlap_test.go")
   596→
   597→	// Verify commit was made
   598→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
   599→	assert.Equal(t, commitsBefore+1, commitsAfter, "Should have one new commit")
   600→
   601→	// Check for checkpoint trailer
   602→	// With content-aware overlap detection, if the user completely replaced
   603→	// the agent's content (new file with different hash), there should be
   604→	// NO checkpoint trailer added. This is documented in checkpoint-scenarios.md
   605→	// under "Content-Aware Overlap Detection".
   606→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   607→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs found: %v", checkpointIDs)
   608→
   609→	// The first commit (Initial commit from NewFeatureBranchEnv) doesn't have a checkpoint.
   610→	// Only agent-assisted commits should have checkpoint trailers.
   611→	// When user completely replaces agent content, content-aware detection should
   612→	// prevent the checkpoint trailer from being added.
   613→	//
   614→	// Per docs/architecture/checkpoint-scenarios.md:
   615→	// - New file + content hash mismatch → No overlap → No checkpoint trailer
   616→	assert.Empty(t, checkpointIDs,
   617→		"Content-aware detection should prevent checkpoint trailer when user completely replaces agent content")
   618→}
   619→
   620→// TestE2E_Scenario1_BasicFlow verifies the simplest workflow matches the documented scenario.
   621→//
   622→// Scenario 1: Prompt → Changes → Prompt Finishes → User Commits
   623→// This test explicitly verifies the documented flow.
   624→func TestE2E_Scenario1_BasicFlow(t *testing.T) {
   625→	t.Parallel()
   626→
   627→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   628→
   629→	// 1. User submits prompt (triggers UserPromptSubmit hook → InitializeSession)
   630→	t.Log("Step 1: User submits prompt")
   631→
   632→	// 2. Claude makes changes (creates files)
   633→	prompt := `Create a file called scenario1.go with this content:
   634→package main
   635→func Scenario1() {}
   636→Create only this file.`
   637→
   638→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   639→	require.NoError(t, err)
   640→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   641→
   642→	// Verify file was created
   643→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("scenario1.go"))
   644→
   645→	// 3. After stop hook: checkpoint is saved, FilesTouched is set
   646→	t.Log("Step 3: Checking rewind points after stop")
   647→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   648→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(points), 1, "Should have rewind point after stop")
   649→	t.Logf("Rewind points: %d", len(points))
   650→
   651→	// 4. User commits (triggers prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks)
   652→	t.Log("Step 4: User commits")
   653→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add scenario1 file", "scenario1.go")
   654→
   655→	// 5. Verify checkpoint was created with trailer
   656→	checkpointID, err := env.GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory()
   657→	require.NoError(t, err, "Should find checkpoint in commit history")
   658→	assert.NotEmpty(t, checkpointID, "Commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
   659→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   660→
   661→	// 6. Verify shadow branch was cleaned up and metadata branch exists
   662→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   663→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   664→
   665→	// 7. Validate checkpoint has proper metadata and transcript
   666→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   667→		CheckpointID:              checkpointID,
   668→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   669→		FilesTouched:              []string{"scenario1.go"},
   670→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"scenario1.go"},
   671→	})
   672→}
   673→
   674→// TestE2E_Scenario2_AgentCommitsDuringTurn verifies the deferred finalization flow.
   675→//
   676→// Scenario 2: Prompt Commits Within Single Turn
   677→// - Agent commits during ACTIVE phase
   678→// - PostCommit saves provisional transcript
   679→// - HandleTurnEnd (Stop hook) finalizes with full transcript
   680→func TestE2E_Scenario2_AgentCommitsDuringTurn(t *testing.T) {
   681→	t.Parallel()
   682→
   683→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   684→
   685→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
   686→
   687→	// Agent creates file and commits it
   688→	t.Log("Agent creating file and committing")
   689→	commitPrompt := `Create a file called agent_commit.go with this content:
   690→package main
   691→func AgentCommit() {}
   692→
   693→Then commit it with: git add agent_commit.go && git commit -m "Agent adds file"
   694→
   695→Create the file first, then run the git commands.`
   696→
   697→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(commitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
   698→	require.NoError(t, err)
   699→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   700→
   701→	// Verify file was created
   702→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_commit.go"))
   703→
   704→	// Verify commit was made
   705→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
   706→	assert.Greater(t, commitsAfter, commitsBefore, "Agent should have made a commit")
   707→
   708→	// Check commit message
   709→	headMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(env.GetHeadHash())
   710→	t.Logf("HEAD commit message: %s", headMsg)
   711→
   712→	// Check for checkpoint trailer
   713→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   714→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   715→
   716→	// Verify metadata branch exists (if checkpoint was created)
   717→	if len(checkpointIDs) > 0 {
   718→		assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   719→			"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   720→
   721→		// Validate checkpoint has proper metadata and transcript
   722→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   723→			CheckpointID:              checkpointIDs[0],
   724→			Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   725→			FilesTouched:              []string{"agent_commit.go"},
   726→			ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"agent_commit.go"},
   727→		})
   728→	}
   729→}
   730→
   731→// =============================================================================
   732→// Tests for MODIFYING EXISTING FILES (not just creating new ones)
   733→// =============================================================================
   734→// These tests ensure checkpoints work correctly when the agent modifies
   735→// tracked files that already exist in the repository, which behaves differently
   736→// from creating new (untracked) files with respect to git stash, staging, etc.
   737→
   738→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_ModifyAndCommit tests basic workflow where agent
   739→// modifies an existing tracked file instead of creating a new one.
   740→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_ModifyAndCommit(t *testing.T) {
   741→	t.Parallel()
   742→
   743→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   744→
   745→	// Create and commit an existing file first
   746→	env.WriteFile("config.go", `package main
   747→
   748→var Config = map[string]string{
   749→	"version": "1.0",
   750→}
   751→`)
   752→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add initial config", "config.go")
   753→
   754→	// Agent modifies the existing file
   755→	t.Log("Agent modifying existing config.go")
   756→	prompt := `Modify the file config.go to add a new config key "debug" with value "true".
   757→Keep the existing content and just add the new key. Only modify this one file.`
   758→
   759→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   760→	require.NoError(t, err)
   761→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   762→
   763→	// Verify file was modified
   764→	content := env.ReadFile("config.go")
   765→	assert.Contains(t, content, "debug", "config.go should contain debug key")
   766→
   767→	// User commits the modification
   768→	t.Log("User committing modified config.go")
   769→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add debug config", "config.go")
   770→
   771→	// Verify checkpoint was created
   772→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   773→	// First commit (initial config) may or may not have checkpoint depending on session state
   774→	// The agent's modification commit should have a checkpoint
   775→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointIDs, "Should have at least one checkpoint")
   776→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   777→}
   778→
   779→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_StashModifications tests stashing modifications to
   780→// tracked files (not new untracked files). This exercises the standard git stash
   781→// behavior (without -u flag) since modifications to tracked files are stashable.
   782→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_StashModifications(t *testing.T) {
   783→	t.Parallel()
   784→
   785→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   786→
   787→	// Create and commit two existing files
   788→	env.WriteFile("fileA.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() { /* original */ }\n")
   789→	env.WriteFile("fileB.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() { /* original */ }\n")
   790→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add initial files", "fileA.go", "fileB.go")
   791→
   792→	// Agent modifies both files
   793→	t.Log("Agent modifying fileA.go and fileB.go")
   794→	prompt := `Modify these files:
   795→1. In fileA.go, change the comment from "original" to "modified by agent"
   796→2. In fileB.go, change the comment from "original" to "modified by agent"
   797→Only modify these two files.`
   798→
   799→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   800→	require.NoError(t, err)
   801→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   802→
   803→	// Verify both files were modified
   804→	contentA := env.ReadFile("fileA.go")
   805→	contentB := env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   806→	assert.Contains(t, contentA, "modified by agent", "fileA.go should be modified")
   807→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should be modified")
   808→
   809→	// User commits only fileA.go
   810→	t.Log("Committing only fileA.go")
   811→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update fileA", "fileA.go")
   812→	checkpoint1 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   813→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint1, "First commit should have checkpoint")
   814→
   815→	// User stashes fileB.go modifications (tracked file, standard stash works)
   816→	t.Log("Stashing fileB.go modifications")
   817→	env.GitStash()
   818→
   819→	// Verify fileB.go is reverted to original
   820→	contentB = env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   821→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "original", "fileB.go should be reverted after stash")
   822→	assert.NotContains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should not have agent changes")
   823→
   824→	// User pops the stash and commits fileB.go
   825→	t.Log("Popping stash and committing fileB.go")
   826→	env.GitStashPop()
   827→	contentB = env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   828→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should have agent changes after stash pop")
   829→
   830→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update fileB", "fileB.go")
   831→	checkpoint2 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   832→
   833→	// Both commits should have checkpoints
   834→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint2, "Second commit should have checkpoint")
   835→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpoint1, checkpoint2, "Checkpoints should be different")
   836→
   837→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s, Checkpoint 2: %s", checkpoint1, checkpoint2)
   838→}
   839→
   840→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_SplitCommits tests user splitting agent's modifications
   841→// to multiple existing files into separate commits.
   842→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_SplitCommits(t *testing.T) {
   843→	t.Parallel()
   844→
   845→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   846→
   847→	// Create and commit multiple existing files
   848→	env.WriteFile("model.go", "package main\n\ntype Model struct{}\n")
   849→	env.WriteFile("view.go", "package main\n\ntype View struct{}\n")
   850→	env.WriteFile("controller.go", "package main\n\ntype Controller struct{}\n")
   851→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add MVC scaffolding", "model.go", "view.go", "controller.go")
   852→
   853→	// Agent modifies all three files
   854→	t.Log("Agent modifying model.go, view.go, controller.go")
   855→	prompt := `Add a Name field (string type) to each struct in these files:
   856→1. model.go - add Name string to Model struct
   857→2. view.go - add Name string to View struct
   858→3. controller.go - add Name string to Controller struct
   859→Only modify these three files.`
   860→
   861→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   862→	require.NoError(t, err)
   863→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   864→
   865→	// User commits each file separately
   866→	t.Log("Committing model.go")
   867→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to Model", "model.go")
   868→	checkpointModel := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   869→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointModel, "Model commit should have checkpoint")
   870→
   871→	t.Log("Committing view.go")
   872→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to View", "view.go")
   873→	checkpointView := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   874→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointView, "View commit should have checkpoint")
   875→
   876→	t.Log("Committing controller.go")
   877→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to Controller", "controller.go")
   878→	checkpointController := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   879→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointController, "Controller commit should have checkpoint")
   880→
   881→	// All three checkpoints should be different
   882→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointModel, checkpointView)
   883→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointView, checkpointController)
   884→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointModel, checkpointController)
   885→
   886→	t.Logf("Checkpoints: model=%s, view=%s, controller=%s",
   887→		checkpointModel, checkpointView, checkpointController)
   888→
   889→	// Validate each checkpoint has the correct files_touched
   890→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   891→		CheckpointID: checkpointModel,
   892→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   893→		FilesTouched: []string{"model.go"},
   894→	})
   895→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   896→		CheckpointID: checkpointView,
   897→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   898→		FilesTouched: []string{"view.go"},
   899→	})
   900→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   901→		CheckpointID: checkpointController,
   902→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   903→		FilesTouched: []string{"controller.go"},
   904→	})
   905→
   906→	// No shadow branches should remain after all files committed
   907→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   908→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   909→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   910→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   911→	}
   912→}
   913→
   914→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_RevertModification tests that modifying an existing file
   915→// that was touched by the session ALWAYS gets a checkpoint, even if the user writes
   916→// completely different content.
   917→//
   918→// This is intentional behavior: for files that already exist in HEAD (modified files),
   919→// we always count as overlap because the user is editing a file the session worked on.
   920→// Content-aware detection only applies to NEW files (don't exist in HEAD).
   921→//
   922→// See content_overlap.go for the rationale.
   923→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_RevertModification(t *testing.T) {
   924→	t.Parallel()
   925→
   926→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   927→
   928→	// Create and commit an existing file
   929→	originalContent := `package main
   930→
   931→// placeholder
   932→`
   933→	env.WriteFile("calc.go", originalContent)
   934→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add placeholder", "calc.go")
   935→
   936→	// Agent modifies the existing file
   937→	t.Log("Agent modifying calc.go")
   938→	prompt := `Replace the contents of calc.go with this exact code:
   939→package main
   940→
   941→func AgentMultiply(a, b int) int {
   942→	return a * b
   943→}
   944→
   945→Only modify calc.go, nothing else.`
   946→
   947→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   948→	require.NoError(t, err)
   949→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   950→
   951→	// Verify agent modified the file
   952→	modifiedContent := env.ReadFile("calc.go")
   953→	assert.Contains(t, modifiedContent, "AgentMultiply", "calc.go should have AgentMultiply function")
   954→
   955→	// User reverts and writes completely different content
   956→	t.Log("User reverting and writing completely different content")
   957→	userContent := `package main
   958→
   959→func UserAdd(x, y int) int {
   960→	return x + y
   961→}
   962→`
   963→	env.WriteFile("calc.go", userContent)
   964→
   965→	// User commits their changes
   966→	t.Log("Committing user's changes")
   967→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user functions", "calc.go")
   968→
   969→	// For MODIFIED files (existing in HEAD), we always count as overlap.
   970→	// The checkpoint SHOULD be created even though content is different.
   971→	// This is because the session touched this file, so any commit to it
   972→	// should be linked to preserve the editing history.
   973→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   974→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   975→
   976→	latestCheckpoint := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   977→	assert.NotEmpty(t, latestCheckpoint,
   978→		"Modified files should always get checkpoint (content-aware only applies to new files)")
   979→}
   980→
   981→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_MixedNewAndModified tests a scenario where the agent
   982→// both creates new files AND modifies existing files in the same session.
   983→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_MixedNewAndModified(t *testing.T) {
   984→	t.Parallel()
   985→
   986→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   987→
   988→	// Create and commit an existing file
   989→	env.WriteFile("main.go", `package main
   990→
   991→func main() {
   992→	// TODO: add imports
   993→}
   994→`)
   995→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
   996→
   997→	// Agent modifies existing file AND creates new files
   998→	t.Log("Agent modifying main.go and creating new files")
   999→	prompt := `Do these tasks:
  1000→1. Create a new file utils.go with: package main; func Helper() string { return "helper" }
  1001→2. Create a new file types.go with: package main; type Config struct { Name string }
  1002→3. Modify main.go to add a comment "// imports utils and types" at the top (after package main)
  1003→Complete all three tasks.`
  1004→
  1005→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1006→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1007→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1008→
  1009→	// Verify all changes
  1010→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("utils.go"), "utils.go should exist")
  1011→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("types.go"), "types.go should exist")
  1012→	mainContent := env.ReadFile("main.go")
  1013→	assert.Contains(t, mainContent, "imports utils and types", "main.go should be modified")
  1014→
  1015→	// User commits existing file modification first
  1016→	t.Log("Committing main.go modification")
  1017→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update main.go imports comment", "main.go")
  1018→	checkpoint1 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
  1019→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint1, "main.go commit should have checkpoint")
  1020→
  1021→	// User commits new files together
  1022→	t.Log("Committing new files utils.go and types.go")
  1023→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add utils and types", "utils.go", "types.go")
  1024→	checkpoint2 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
  1025→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint2, "New files commit should have checkpoint")
  1026→
  1027→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpoint1, checkpoint2, "Checkpoints should be different")
  1028→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 (modified): %s, Checkpoint 2 (new): %s", checkpoint1, checkpoint2)
  1029→}
  1030→
  1031→// TestE2E_EndedSession_UserCommitsAfterExit tests that after agent exits
  1032→// (session naturally ends), user commits still get checkpoint trailers.
  1033→// This exercises the ENDED + GitCommit → ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched path.
  1034→func TestE2E_EndedSession_UserCommitsAfterExit(t *testing.T) {
  1035→	t.Parallel()
  1036→
  1037→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1038→
  1039→	// Agent creates files A, B, C — session ends when agent exits
  1040→	prompt := `Create these files:
  1041→1. ended_a.go with content: package main; func EndedA() {}
  1042→2. ended_b.go with content: package main; func EndedB() {}
  1043→3. ended_c.go with content: package main; func EndedC() {}
  1044→Create all three files, nothing else.`
  1045→
  1046→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1047→	require.NoError(t, err)

  1051→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("ended_b.go"), "ended_b.go should exist")
  1052→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("ended_c.go"), "ended_c.go should exist")

  1056→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add ended files A and B", "ended_a.go", "ended_b.go")

  1094→// TestE2E_DeletedFiles_CommitDeletion tests that deleting a file that was tracked
  1095→// in the session gets handled properly when committed via git rm.
  1096→func TestE2E_DeletedFiles_CommitDeletion(t *testing.T) {
  1097→	t.Parallel()
  1098→
  1099→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1100→
  1101→	// Pre-commit a file that will be deleted
  1102→	env.WriteFile("to_delete.go", "package main\n\nfunc ToDelete() {}\n")
  1103→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add to_delete.go", "to_delete.go")
  1104→
  1105→	// Agent deletes it and creates a replacement
  1106→	prompt := `Do these two tasks:
  1107→1. Delete the file to_delete.go (use: rm to_delete.go)
  1108→2. Create a new file replacement.go with content: package main; func Replacement() {}
  1109→
  1110→Do both tasks.`
  1111→
  1112→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
  1113→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1114→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1115→
  1116→	// to_delete.go should be gone, replacement.go should exist
  1117→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("to_delete.go"), "to_delete.go should be deleted")
  1118→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("replacement.go"), "replacement.go should exist")
  1119→
  1120→	// Commit the replacement file first
  1121→	t.Log("Committing replacement.go")
  1122→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add replacement", "replacement.go")
  1123→	checkpointIDsAfterFirst := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1124→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterFirst), 1, "Should have checkpoint for replacement")
  1125→	checkpointReplacement := checkpointIDsAfterFirst[0]
  1126→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for replacement: %s", checkpointReplacement)
  1127→
  1128→	// Commit the deletion via git rm
  1129→	t.Log("Committing deletion of to_delete.go")
  1130→	env.GitRm("to_delete.go")
  1131→	env.GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks("Remove to_delete.go")
  1132→
  1133→	// Check if deletion got a checkpoint trailer
  1134→	checkpointIDsAfterSecond := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1135→	if len(checkpointIDsAfterSecond) > len(checkpointIDsAfterFirst) {
  1136→		checkpointDeletion := checkpointIDsAfterSecond[0]
  1137→		t.Logf("Checkpoint for deletion: %s (carry-forward for deleted file)", checkpointDeletion)
  1138→		assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointReplacement, checkpointDeletion,
  1139→			"Checkpoint IDs should be unique")
  1140→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1141→			CheckpointID: checkpointDeletion,
  1142→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1143→		})
  1144→	} else {
  1145→		t.Log("Deletion commit has no checkpoint trailer (deleted files may not carry forward)")
  1146→	}
  1147→
  1148→	// Validate the replacement checkpoint
  1149→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1150→		CheckpointID: checkpointReplacement,
  1151→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1152→	})
  1153→}

  1155→// TestE2E_AgentCommitsMidTurn_UserCommitsRemainder tests that when the agent
  1156→// commits some files during its turn and the user commits the rest after,
  1157→// both get valid checkpoint trailers.
  1158→func TestE2E_AgentCommitsMidTurn_UserCommitsRemainder(t *testing.T) {
  1159→	t.Parallel()
  1160→
  1161→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1162→
  1163→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
  1164→
  1165→	// Agent creates 3 files and commits 2 mid-turn
  1166→	prompt := `Do these tasks in order:
  1167→1. Create file agent_mid1.go with content: package main; func AgentMid1() {}
  1168→2. Create file agent_mid2.go with content: package main; func AgentMid2() {}
  1169→3. Commit these two files: git add agent_mid1.go agent_mid2.go && git commit -m "Agent adds mid1 and mid2"
  1170→4. Create file user_remainder.go with content: package main; func UserRemainder() {}
  1171→
  1172→Do all tasks in order. Create each file, then commit the first two, then create the third.`
  1173→
  1174→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
  1175→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1176→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1177→
  1178→	// Verify all files were created
  1179→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_mid1.go"), "agent_mid1.go should exist")
  1180→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_mid2.go"), "agent_mid2.go should exist")
  1181→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("user_remainder.go"), "user_remainder.go should exist")
  1182→
  1183→	// Verify agent made a commit

  1185→	assert.Greater(t, commitsAfter, commitsBefore, "Agent should have made a commit")
  1186→
  1187→	// Get agent checkpoint IDs

  1189→	t.Logf("Agent checkpoint IDs: %v", agentCheckpointIDs)
  1190→
  1191→	// User commits the remaining file
  1192→	t.Log("User committing user_remainder.go")
  1193→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user remainder", "user_remainder.go")
  1194→
  1195→	// Get all checkpoint IDs including user's
  1196→	allCheckpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1197→	t.Logf("All checkpoint IDs: %v", allCheckpointIDs)
  1198→
  1199→	// User's checkpoint should exist and be different from agent's
  1200→	require.Greater(t, len(allCheckpointIDs), len(agentCheckpointIDs),
  1201→		"User commit should have added a new checkpoint")
  1202→
  1203→	userCheckpointID := allCheckpointIDs[0] // Most recent
  1204→	t.Logf("User checkpoint ID: %s", userCheckpointID)
  1205→
  1206→	// User checkpoint must be different from all agent checkpoints
  1207→	for _, agentID := range agentCheckpointIDs {
  1208→		assert.NotEqual(t, userCheckpointID, agentID,
  1209→			"User checkpoint should be different from agent checkpoint")
  1210→	}
  1211→
  1212→	// Validate user's checkpoint exists on metadata branch
  1213→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1214→		CheckpointID: userCheckpointID,
  1215→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1216→		FilesTouched: []string{"user_remainder.go"},
  1217→	})
  1218→
  1219→	// No shadow branches should remain
  1220→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
  1221→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
  1222→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
  1223→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
  1224→	}
  1225→}

  1227→// TestE2E_TrailerRemoval_SkipsCondensation tests that when a user removes the
  1228→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer from the commit message, no condensation happens.
  1229→func TestE2E_TrailerRemoval_SkipsCondensation(t *testing.T) {
  1230→	t.Parallel()
  1231→
  1232→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1233→
  1234→	// Agent creates a file

  1236→package main
  1237→func TrailerTest() {}
  1238→Create only this file.`
  1239→
  1240→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1241→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1242→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1243→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("trailer_test.go"), "trailer_test.go should exist")
  1244→
  1245→	// Count checkpoints before
  1246→	checkpointsBefore := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1247→
  1248→	// User commits with trailer removed (simulating user editing out the trailer)
  1249→	t.Log("Committing with trailer removed")
  1250→	env.GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved("Add trailer_test (no checkpoint)", "trailer_test.go")
  1251→
  1252→	// Verify the commit message does NOT have the trailer
  1253→	headMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(env.GetHeadHash())
  1254→	assert.NotContains(t, headMsg, "Entire-Checkpoint:",
  1255→		"Commit message should not have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
  1256→
  1257→	// No new checkpoint should have been created

  1259→	assert.Len(t, checkpointsAfter, len(checkpointsBefore),
  1260→		"No new checkpoint should have been created when trailer is removed")
  1261→}

> AGENT

The test shows that checkpoint IDs are being added to commits (3 found), but the metadata is not being condensed to `entire/checkpoints/v1`. This means the `prepare-commit-msg` hook is working (adding trailers) but the `post-commit` hook is not running (condensation).

Let me look at how OpenCode hooks work versus Claude Code hooks.

     1→// Package opencode implements the Agent interface for OpenCode.
     2→package opencode
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"

    19→
    20→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    21→func init() {
    22→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameOpenCode, NewOpenCodeAgent)
    23→}
    24→
    25→//nolint:revive // OpenCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    26→type OpenCodeAgent struct{}
    27→
    28→// NewOpenCodeAgent creates a new OpenCode agent instance.
    29→func NewOpenCodeAgent() agent.Agent {
    30→	return &OpenCodeAgent{}
    31→}
    32→
    33→// --- Identity ---
    34→
    35→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName   { return agent.AgentNameOpenCode }
    36→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return agent.AgentTypeOpenCode }
    37→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Description() string     { return "OpenCode - AI-powered terminal coding agent" }
    38→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) IsPreview() bool         { return true }
    39→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".opencode"} }
    40→
    41→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    42→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    43→	if err != nil {
    44→		repoRoot = "."
    45→	}
    46→	// Check for .opencode directory or opencode.json config
    47→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".opencode")); err == nil {
    48→		return true, nil
    49→	}
    50→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, "opencode.json")); err == nil {
    51→		return true, nil
    52→	}
    53→	return false, nil
    54→}
    55→
    56→// --- Transcript Storage ---
    57→
    58→// ReadTranscript reads the transcript for a session.
    59→// The sessionRef is expected to be a path to the export JSON file.
    60→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    61→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    62→	if err != nil {
    63→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read opencode transcript: %w", err)
    64→	}
    65→	return data, nil
    66→}
    67→
    68→// ChunkTranscript splits an OpenCode export JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
    69→// OpenCode uses JSON format with {"info": {...}, "messages": [...]} structure.
    70→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    71→	var session ExportSession
    72→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &session); err != nil {
    73→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse export session for chunking: %w", err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	if len(session.Messages) == 0 {
    77→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
    78→	}
    79→
    80→	// Marshal info to calculate accurate base size
    81→	infoBytes, err := json.Marshal(session.Info)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session info for chunking: %w", err)
    84→	}
    85→	// Base JSON structure size: {"info":<info>,"messages":[ ... ]}
    86→	baseSize := len(`{"info":`) + len(infoBytes) + len(`,"messages":[]}`)
    87→
    88→	var chunks [][]byte
    89→	var currentMessages []ExportMessage
    90→	currentSize := baseSize
    91→
    92→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
    93→		// Marshal message to get its size
    94→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
    95→		if err != nil {
    96→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal message for chunking: %w", err)
    97→		}
    98→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
    99→
   100→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   101→			// Save current chunk
   102→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(ExportSession{Info: session.Info, Messages: currentMessages})
   103→			if err != nil {
   104→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   105→			}
   106→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   107→
   108→			// Start new chunk
   109→			currentMessages = nil
   110→			currentSize = baseSize
   111→		}
   112→
   113→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   114→		currentSize += msgSize
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Add the last chunk
   118→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   119→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(ExportSession{Info: session.Info, Messages: currentMessages})
   120→		if err != nil {
   121→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   122→		}
   123→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   127→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks")
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	return chunks, nil
   131→}
   132→
   133→// ReassembleTranscript merges OpenCode export JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   134→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   135→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   136→		return nil, errors.New("no chunks to reassemble")
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	var allMessages []ExportMessage
   140→	var sessionInfo SessionInfo
   141→
   142→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   143→		var session ExportSession
   144→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &session); err != nil {
   145→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk %d: %w", i, err)
   146→		}
   147→		if i == 0 {
   148→			sessionInfo = session.Info // Preserve session info from first chunk
   149→		}
   150→		allMessages = append(allMessages, session.Messages...)
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	result, err := json.Marshal(ExportSession{Info: sessionInfo, Messages: allMessages})
   154→	if err != nil {
   155→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	return result, nil
   158→}
   159→
   160→// --- Legacy methods ---
   161→
   162→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   163→	return input.SessionID
   164→}
   165→
   166→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Entire stores OpenCode session transcripts.
   167→// Transcripts are ephemeral handoff files between the TS plugin and the Go hook handler.
   168→// Once checkpointed, the data lives on git refs and the file is disposable.
   169→// Stored in os.TempDir()/entire-opencode/<sanitized-path>/ to avoid squatting on
   170→// OpenCode's own directories (~/.opencode/ is project-level, not home-level).
   171→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   172→	// Check for test environment override
   173→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   174→		return override, nil
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	projectDir := SanitizePathForOpenCode(repoPath)
   178→	return filepath.Join(os.TempDir(), "entire-opencode", projectDir), nil
   179→}
   180→
   181→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   186→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   187→		return nil, errors.New("no session ref provided")
   188→	}
   189→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   190→	if err != nil {
   191→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session: %w", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   195→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   196→	if err != nil {
   197→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   198→		modifiedFiles = nil
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   202→		AgentName:     a.Name(),
   203→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   204→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   205→		NativeData:    data,
   206→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   207→	}, nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("nil session")
   213→	}
   214→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   215→		return errors.New("no session data to write")
   216→	}
   217→
   218→	// Import the session into OpenCode's database.
   219→	// This enables `opencode -s <id>` for both resume and rewind.
   220→	return a.importSessionIntoOpenCode(session.SessionID, session.NativeData)
   221→}
   222→
   223→// importSessionIntoOpenCode writes the export JSON to a temp file and runs
   224→// `opencode import` to restore the session into OpenCode's database.
   225→// For rewind (session already exists), the session is deleted first so the
   226→// reimport replaces it with the checkpoint-state messages.
   227→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) importSessionIntoOpenCode(sessionID string, exportData []byte) error {
   228→	// Delete existing session first so reimport replaces it cleanly.
   229→	// opencode import uses ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, so existing messages
   230→	// would be skipped without this step (breaking rewind).
   231→	if err := runOpenCodeSessionDelete(sessionID); err != nil {
   232→		// Non-fatal: session might not exist yet (first session).
   233→		// Import will still work for new sessions; only rewind of existing sessions
   234→		// would have stale messages.
   235→		logging.Warn(context.Background(), "could not delete existing opencode session",
   236→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   237→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   238→		)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	// Write export JSON to a temp file for opencode import
   242→	tmpFile, err := os.CreateTemp("", "entire-opencode-export-*.json")
   243→	if err != nil {
   244→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp file: %w", err)
   245→	}
   246→	defer os.Remove(tmpFile.Name())
   247→
   248→	if _, err := tmpFile.Write(exportData); err != nil {
   249→		_ = tmpFile.Close()
   250→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write export data: %w", err)
   251→	}
   252→	if err := tmpFile.Close(); err != nil {
   253→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to close temp file: %w", err)
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	return runOpenCodeImport(tmpFile.Name())
   257→}
   258→
   259→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   260→	if strings.TrimSpace(sessionID) == "" {
   261→		return "opencode"
   262→	}
   263→	return "opencode -s " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character.
   267→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   268→
   269→// SanitizePathForOpenCode converts a path to a safe directory name.
   270→// Replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash (same approach as Claude/Gemini).
   271→func SanitizePathForOpenCode(path string) string {
   272→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   273→}
   274→

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"os"
     6→	"path/filepath"
     7→	"strings"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Compile-time interface assertion
    14→var _ agent.HookSupport = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    15→
    16→const (
    17→	// pluginFileName is the name of the plugin file written to .opencode/plugins/
    18→	pluginFileName = "entire.ts"
    19→
    20→	// pluginDirName is the directory under .opencode/ where plugins live
    21→	pluginDirName = "plugins"
    22→
    23→	// entireMarker is a string present in the plugin file to identify it as Entire's
    24→	entireMarker = "Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`"
    25→)
    26→
    27→// getPluginPath returns the absolute path to the plugin file.
    28→func getPluginPath() (string, error) {
    29→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    32→		//nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    33→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd()
    34→		if err != nil {
    35→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    36→		}
    37→	}
    38→	return filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".opencode", pluginDirName, pluginFileName), nil
    39→}
    40→
    41→// InstallHooks writes the Entire plugin file to .opencode/plugins/entire.ts.
    42→// Returns 1 if the plugin was installed, 0 if already present (idempotent).
    43→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    44→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return 0, err
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	// Check if already installed (idempotent) unless force
    50→	if !force {
    51→		if _, err := os.Stat(pluginPath); err == nil {
    52→			data, readErr := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
    53→			if readErr == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker) {
    54→				return 0, nil // Already installed
    55→			}
    56→		}
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Build the command prefix
    60→	var cmdPrefix string
    61→	if localDev {
    62→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${OPENCODE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go"
    63→	} else {
    64→		cmdPrefix = "entire"
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Generate plugin content from template
    68→	content := strings.ReplaceAll(pluginTemplate, entireCmdPlaceholder, cmdPrefix)
    69→
    70→	// Ensure directory exists
    71→	pluginDir := filepath.Dir(pluginPath)
    72→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Plugin directory needs standard permissions
    73→	if err := os.MkdirAll(pluginDir, 0o755); err != nil {
    74→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create plugin directory: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Write plugin file
    78→	//nolint:gosec // G306: Plugin file needs standard permissions for OpenCode to read
    79→	if err := os.WriteFile(pluginPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
    80→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write plugin file: %w", err)
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	return 1, nil
    84→}
    85→
    86→// UninstallHooks removes the Entire plugin file.
    87→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
    88→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return err
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	if err := os.Remove(pluginPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    94→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove plugin file: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	return nil
    98→}
    99→
   100→// AreHooksInstalled checks if the Entire plugin file exists and contains the marker.
   101→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   102→	pluginPath, err := getPluginPath()
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return false
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	data, err := os.ReadFile(pluginPath) //nolint:gosec // Path constructed from repo root
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		return false
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	return strings.Contains(string(data), entireMarker)
   113→}
   114→
   115→// GetSupportedHooks returns the normalized lifecycle events this agent supports.
   116→// OpenCode's native hooks map to standard agent lifecycle events:
   117→//   - session-start → HookSessionStart
   118→//   - session-end   → HookSessionEnd
   119→//   - turn-start    → HookUserPromptSubmit (user prompt triggers a turn)
   120→//   - turn-end      → HookStop (agent response complete)
   121→//
   122→// Note: HookNames() returns 5 hooks (including "compaction"), but GetSupportedHooks()
   123→// returns only 4. The "compaction" hook is OpenCode-specific with no standard HookType
   124→// mapping — it is handled via ParseHookEvent but not advertised as a standard lifecycle event.
   125→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   126→	return []agent.HookType{
   127→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   128→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   129→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   130→		agent.HookStop,
   131→	}
   132→}

    24→// defaultAgent holds the agent to test with, determined in TestMain.
    25→var defaultAgent string
    26→
    27→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    28→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.

    30→
    31→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for E2E tests with real agent calls.
    32→type TestEnv struct {
    33→	T       *testing.T
    34→	RepoDir string
    35→	Agent   AgentRunner
    36→}
    37→
    38→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    39→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    40→func getTestBinary() string {
    41→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    42→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    43→	}
    44→	return testBinaryPath
    45→}
    46→
    47→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated E2E test environment.
    48→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    49→	t.Helper()
    50→
    51→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    52→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    53→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    54→		repoDir = resolved
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Create agent runner
    58→	agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    59→
    60→	return &TestEnv{
    61→		T:       t,
    62→		RepoDir: repoDir,
    63→		Agent:   agent,
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→	t.Helper()
    72→
    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→
    80→	// OpenCode's `run` (non-interactive) mode defaults external_directory permission
    81→	// to "ask", which auto-rejects since there's no user to prompt — even when the
    82→	// path is the project directory itself. Override to "allow" for the test repo.
    83→	// Note: specific path patterns don't work here because OpenCode evaluates the
    84→	// catch-all "ask" rule before specific "allow" rules (known issue).
    85→	// Include $schema to prevent OpenCode from modifying the file when it runs.
    86→	if defaultAgent == AgentNameOpenCode {
    87→		env.WriteFile("opencode.json", `{
    88→  "$schema": "https://opencode.ai/config.json",
    89→  "permission": {
    90→    "external_directory": "allow"
    91→  }
    92→}
    93→`)
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    97→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    98→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    99→
   100→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
   101→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
   102→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
   103→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
   104→	env.GitAddAll()
   105→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
   106→
   107→	return env
   108→}
   109→
   110→// RunEntireEnable runs `entire enable` to set up the project with hooks.
   111→// Uses the configured defaultAgent (from E2E_AGENT env var or "claude-code").
   112→func (env *TestEnv) RunEntireEnable(strategyName string) {
   113→	env.T.Helper()
   114→
   115→	args := []string{
   116→		"enable",
   117→		"--agent", defaultAgent,
   118→		"--strategy", strategyName,
   119→		"--telemetry=false",
   120→		"--force", // Force reinstall hooks in case they exist
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static
   124→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), args...)
   125→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   126→
   127→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		env.T.Fatalf("entire enable failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   130→	}
   131→	env.T.Logf("entire enable output: %s", output)
   132→}
   133→
   134→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.
   135→func (env *TestEnv) InitRepo() {
   136→	env.T.Helper()
   137→
   138→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Configure git user for commits
   144→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   145→	if err != nil {
   146→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   147→	}
   148→	cfg.User.Name = "E2E Test User"
   149→	cfg.User.Email = "e2e-test@example.com"
   150→
   151→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   152→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   153→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   154→	}
   155→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   156→
   157→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   158→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   159→	}
   160→}
   161→
   162→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.

   166→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   167→

   169→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   170→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   171→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   172→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   176→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   177→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   178→	}
   179→}

   216→// GitAdd stages files for commit.
   217→func (env *TestEnv) GitAdd(paths ...string) {
   218→	env.T.Helper()
   219→
   220→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   221→	if err != nil {

   223→	}
   224→

   228→	}

   231→		if _, err := worktree.Add(path); err != nil {
   232→			env.T.Fatalf("failed to add file %s: %v", path, err)
   233→		}
   234→	}
   235→}

   237→// GitAddAll stages all files (git add .).
   238→func (env *TestEnv) GitAddAll() {

   241→	cmd := exec.Command("git", "add", ".")
   242→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir

   244→		env.T.Fatalf("git add . failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)

   248→// GitCommit creates a commit with all staged files.
   249→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommit(message string) {
   250→	env.T.Helper()
   251→
   252→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   255→	}

   257→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()

   260→	}
   261→
   262→	_, err = worktree.Commit(message, &git.CommitOptions{

   268→	})
