   273→
   274→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, running the prepare-commit-msg
   275→// and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   276→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   277→	env.T.Helper()
   278→
   279→	// Stage files using go-git
   280→	for _, file := range files {
   281→		env.GitAdd(file)
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   285→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   286→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   287→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   288→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   292→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   293→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   294→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   295→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   296→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   297→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   298→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   299→	)
   300→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   301→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	// Read the modified message
   305→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   306→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   312→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   323→		Author: &object.Signature{
   324→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   325→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   326→			When:  time.Now(),
   327→		},
   328→	})
   329→	if err != nil {
   330→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	// Run post-commit hook
   334→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   335→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   336→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   337→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   338→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   339→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   340→	)
   341→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   342→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   343→	}
   344→}
   345→
   346→// GitRm stages file deletions using git rm.
   347→func (env *TestEnv) GitRm(paths ...string) {
   348→	env.T.Helper()
   349→
   350→	args := append([]string{"rm", "--"}, paths...)
   351→	//nolint:gosec // test code, args are from trusted test setup
   352→	cmd := exec.Command("git", args...)

   358→
   359→// GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks commits whatever is already staged (without adding files first),
   360→// running the prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   361→// Use this after GitRm or when files are already staged.
   362→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks(message string) {
   363→	env.T.Helper()
   364→
   365→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   366→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   367→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   368→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   369→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   373→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   374→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   375→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   376→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   377→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   378→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   379→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   380→	)
   381→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   382→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	// Read the modified message
   386→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   387→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   388→	if err != nil {
   389→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   393→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   394→	if err != nil {
   395→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   401→	}
   402→
   403→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   404→		Author: &object.Signature{
   405→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   406→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   407→			When:  time.Now(),
   408→		},
   409→	})
   410→	if err != nil {
   411→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   412→	}
   413→
   414→	// Run post-commit hook
   415→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   416→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   417→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   418→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   419→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   420→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   421→	)
   422→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   423→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   424→	}
   425→}
   426→
   427→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
   428→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
   429→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
   430→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
   431→	env.T.Helper()
   432→
   433→	// Stage files using go-git
   434→	for _, file := range files {
   435→		env.GitAdd(file)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   439→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   440→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   441→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   442→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   446→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   447→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   448→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   449→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   450→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   451→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   452→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   453→	)
   454→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   455→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
   459→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   460→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   461→	if err != nil {
   462→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
   466→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
   467→	var cleanedLines []string
   468→	for _, line := range lines {
   469→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   470→			continue
   471→		}
   472→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
   473→			continue
   474→		}
   475→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
   476→			continue
   477→		}
   478→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
   479→	}
   480→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
   481→
   482→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
   483→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   484→	if err != nil {
   485→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   489→	if err != nil {
   490→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   494→		Author: &object.Signature{
   495→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   496→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   497→			When:  time.Now(),
   498→		},
   499→	})
   500→	if err != nil {
   501→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
   505→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   506→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   507→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   508→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   509→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   510→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   511→	)
   512→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   513→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   514→	}
   515→}

   546→// RewindPoint mirrors the rewind --list JSON output.
   547→type RewindPoint struct {
   548→	ID               string    `json:"id"`
   549→	Message          string    `json:"message"`
   550→	MetadataDir      string    `json:"metadata_dir"`
   551→	Date             time.Time `json:"date"`
   552→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool      `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   553→	ToolUseID        string    `json:"tool_use_id"`
   554→	IsLogsOnly       bool      `json:"is_logs_only"`
   555→	CondensationID   string    `json:"condensation_id"`
   556→}
   557→
   558→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   559→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   560→	env.T.Helper()
   561→
   562→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static, no context needed
   563→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   564→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   565→
   566→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   567→	if err != nil {
   568→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	// Parse JSON output
   572→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   573→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   574→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   575→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   576→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   577→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   578→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   579→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   580→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   584→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   588→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   589→		//nolint:errcheck // date parsing failure is acceptable, defaults to zero time
   590→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   591→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   592→			ID:               jp.ID,
   593→			Message:          jp.Message,
   594→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   595→			Date:             date,
   596→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   597→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   598→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   599→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   600→		}
   601→	}
   602→
   603→	return points
   604→}

   623→// BranchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.
   624→func (env *TestEnv) BranchExists(branchName string) bool {
   625→	env.T.Helper()
   626→
   627→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   628→	if err != nil {
   629→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	refs, err := repo.References()
   633→	if err != nil {
   634→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   635→	}
   636→
   637→	found := false
   638→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   639→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   640→		if ref.Name().Short() == branchName {
   641→			found = true
   642→		}
   643→		return nil
   644→	})
   645→
   646→	return found
   647→}

   649→// ListBranchesWithPrefix returns all branches that start with the given prefix.
   650→func (env *TestEnv) ListBranchesWithPrefix(prefix string) []string {
   651→	env.T.Helper()

   659→	if err != nil {
   660→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	var branches []string
   664→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   665→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   666→		name := ref.Name().Short()
   667→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   668→			branches = append(branches, name)
   669→		}
   670→		return nil
   671→	})
   672→
   673→	return branches
   674→}

   693→
   694→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   695→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   696→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   697→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   698→	env.T.Helper()
   699→
   700→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   701→	if err != nil {
   702→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   703→	}
   704→
   705→	head, err := repo.Head()
   706→	if err != nil {
   707→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   711→	if err != nil {
   712→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   713→	}
   714→
   715→	var checkpointID string
   716→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   717→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   718→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   719→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   720→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   721→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   722→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   723→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   724→			}
   725→		}
   726→		return nil
   727→	})
   728→
   729→	if checkpointID == "" {
   730→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	return checkpointID, nil
   734→}
   735→
   736→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   737→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   738→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   739→	env.T.Helper()
   740→
   741→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   742→	if err != nil {
   743→		return ""
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	head, err := repo.Head()
   747→	if err != nil {
   748→		return ""
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   752→	if err != nil {
   753→		return ""
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   757→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   758→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   759→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   760→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   761→		}
   762→	}
   763→	return ""
   764→}
   765→
   766→// safeIDPrefix returns first 12 chars of ID or the full ID if shorter.
   767→// Use this when logging checkpoint IDs to avoid index out of bounds panic.
   768→func safeIDPrefix(id string) string {
   769→	if len(id) >= 12 {
   770→		return id[:12]
   771→	}
   772→	return id
   773→}

   878→// GetCommitCount returns the number of commits on the current branch.
   879→func (env *TestEnv) GetCommitCount() int {
   880→	env.T.Helper()
   881→
   882→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   883→	if err != nil {
   884→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   885→	}
   886→
   887→	head, err := repo.Head()
   888→	if err != nil {
   889→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   890→	}
   891→
   892→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   893→	if err != nil {
   894→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   895→	}
   896→
   897→	count := 0
   898→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   899→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   900→		count++
   901→		return nil
   902→	})
   903→
   904→	return count
   905→}
   906→
   907→// GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   908→// all checkpoint IDs from commits with Entire-Checkpoint trailers.
   909→func (env *TestEnv) GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory() []string {
   910→	env.T.Helper()
   911→
   912→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   913→	if err != nil {
   914→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   915→	}
   916→
   917→	head, err := repo.Head()
   918→	if err != nil {
   919→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   920→	}
   921→
   922→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   923→	if err != nil {
   924→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   925→	}
   926→
   927→	var checkpointIDs []string
   928→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   929→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   930→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   931→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   932→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   933→				id := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   934→				checkpointIDs = append(checkpointIDs, id)
   935→			}
   936→		}
   937→		return nil
   938→	})
   939→
   940→	return checkpointIDs
   941→}
   942→
   943→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   944→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   945→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   946→	env.T.Helper()
   947→
   948→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   949→	if err != nil {
   950→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   951→	}
   952→
   953→	// Get the branch reference
   954→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   955→	if err != nil {
   956→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   957→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   958→		if err != nil {
   959→			return "", false
   960→		}
   961→	}
   962→
   963→	// Get the commit
   964→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   965→	if err != nil {
   966→		return "", false
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	// Get the tree
   970→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   971→	if err != nil {
   972→		return "", false
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Get the file
   976→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   977→	if err != nil {
   978→		return "", false
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	// Get the content
   982→	content, err := file.Contents()
   983→	if err != nil {
   984→		return "", false
   985→	}
   986→
   987→	return content, true
   988→}

  1008→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
  1009→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1010→	env.T.Helper()
  1011→
  1012→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
  1013→
  1014→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1015→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1016→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1017→	}
  1018→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1019→
  1020→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1021→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1022→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1023→	if !found {
  1024→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1025→	} else {
  1026→		var summary map[string]any
  1027→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1028→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1029→		} else {
  1030→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1031→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1032→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1033→			}
  1034→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1035→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1036→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1037→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1038→				}
  1039→			}
  1040→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1041→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1042→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1043→			}
  1044→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1045→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1046→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1047→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1048→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1049→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1050→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1051→						}
  1052→					}
  1053→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1054→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1055→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1056→						}
  1057→					}
  1058→				}
  1059→			}
  1060→		}
  1061→	}
  1062→
  1063→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1064→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1065→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1066→	if !found {
  1067→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1068→	} else {
  1069→		var metadata map[string]any
  1070→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1071→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1072→		} else {
  1073→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1074→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1075→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1076→			}
  1077→			// Validate created_at exists
  1078→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1079→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1080→			}
  1081→		}
  1082→	}
  1083→
  1084→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1085→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1086→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1087→	if !found {
  1088→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1089→	} else {
  1090→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1091→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1092→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1093→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1094→		validLines := 0
  1095→		for i, line := range lines {
  1096→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1097→			if line == "" {
  1098→				continue
  1099→			}
  1100→			validLines++
  1101→			var obj map[string]any
  1102→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1103→				if isClaudeCode {
  1104→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1105→				}
  1106→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1107→			}
  1108→		}
  1109→		if validLines == 0 {
  1110→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1111→		}
  1112→		// Check expected content
  1113→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1114→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1115→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1116→			}
  1117→		}
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1121→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1122→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1123→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1124→		if !found {
  1125→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1126→		} else {
  1127→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1128→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1129→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1130→				}
  1131→			}
  1132→		}
  1133→	}
  1134→
  1135→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1136→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1137→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1138→	if !found {
  1139→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1140→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1141→		// Verify hash matches
  1142→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1143→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1144→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1145→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1146→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1147→		}
  1148→	}
  1149→}

I see the issue now. Looking at line 293-299 and 337-340, `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` sets environment variables for Claude and Gemini but NOT for OpenCode. But more importantly, the test `TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits` has the **agent** making commits directly via bash (`git add && git commit`), not the test framework.

When OpenCode runs `git commit`, the real git hooks fire. Let me check how those git hooks are set up and if they can find OpenCode's session data.

    15→// Hook marker used to identify Entire CLI hooks
    16→const entireHookMarker = "Entire CLI hooks"
    17→
    18→const backupSuffix = ".pre-entire"
    19→const chainComment = "# Chain: run pre-existing hook"
    20→
    21→// gitHookNames are the git hooks managed by Entire CLI

    23→
    24→// ManagedGitHookNames returns the list of git hooks managed by Entire CLI.
    25→// This is useful for tests that need to manipulate hooks.
    26→func ManagedGitHookNames() []string {
    27→	return gitHookNames
    28→}
    29→
    30→// hookSpec defines a git hook's name and content template (without chain call).
    31→type hookSpec struct {
    32→	name    string
    33→	content string
    34→}
    35→
    36→// GetGitDir returns the actual git directory path by delegating to git itself.
    37→// This handles both regular repositories and worktrees, and inherits git's
    38→// security validation for gitdir references.
    39→func GetGitDir() (string, error) {
    40→	return getGitDirInPath(".")
    41→}
    42→
    43→// GetHooksDir returns the active hooks directory path.
    44→// This respects core.hooksPath and correctly resolves to the common hooks
    45→// directory when called from a linked worktree.
    46→func GetHooksDir() (string, error) {
    47→	return getHooksDirInPath(".")
    48→}
    49→
    50→// getGitDirInPath returns the git directory for a repository at the given path.
    51→// It delegates to `git rev-parse --git-dir` to leverage git's own validation.
    52→func getGitDirInPath(dir string) (string, error) {
    53→	ctx := context.Background()
    54→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-dir")
    55→	cmd.Dir = dir
    56→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return "", errors.New("not a git repository")
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	gitDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    62→
    63→	// git rev-parse --git-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
    64→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
    65→	if !filepath.IsAbs(gitDir) {
    66→		gitDir = filepath.Join(dir, gitDir)
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	return filepath.Clean(gitDir), nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// getHooksDirInPath returns the active hooks directory for a repository at the given path.
    73→// It delegates to `git rev-parse --git-path hooks` so Git resolves:
    74→// - linked-worktree common hooks directory
    75→// - core.hooksPath (relative or absolute)
    76→func getHooksDirInPath(dir string) (string, error) {
    77→	ctx := context.Background()
    78→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-path", "hooks")
    79→	cmd.Dir = dir
    80→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", errors.New("not a git repository")
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	hooksDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    86→	if !filepath.IsAbs(hooksDir) {
    87→		hooksDir = filepath.Join(dir, hooksDir)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	return filepath.Clean(hooksDir), nil
    91→}

   102→// IsGitHookInstalledInDir checks if all Entire CLI hooks are installed in the given repo directory.
   103→// This is useful for tests that need to check hooks without changing the working directory.
   104→func IsGitHookInstalledInDir(repoDir string) bool {
   105→	hooksDir, err := getHooksDirInPath(repoDir)
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return false
   108→	}
   109→	return isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir(hooksDir)
   110→}
   111→
   112→// isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir checks if all hooks are installed in the given hooks directory.
   113→func isGitHookInstalledInHooksDir(hooksDir string) bool {
   114→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   115→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook)
   116→		data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // Path is constructed from constants
   117→		if err != nil {
   118→			return false
   119→		}
   120→		if !strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker) {
   121→			return false
   122→		}
   123→	}
   124→	return true
   125→}

   127→// buildHookSpecs returns the hook specifications for all managed hooks.
   128→func buildHookSpecs(cmdPrefix string) []hookSpec {
   129→	return []hookSpec{
   130→		{
   131→			name: "prepare-commit-msg",
   132→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   133→# %s
   134→%s hooks git prepare-commit-msg "$1" "$2" 2>/dev/null || true
   135→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   136→		},
   137→		{
   138→			name: "commit-msg",
   139→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   140→# %s
   141→# Commit-msg hook: strip trailer if no user content (allows aborting empty commits)
   142→%s hooks git commit-msg "$1" || exit 1
   143→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   144→		},
   145→		{
   146→			name: "post-commit",
   147→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   148→# %s
   149→# Post-commit hook: condense session data if commit has Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   150→%s hooks git post-commit 2>/dev/null || true
   151→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   152→		},
   153→		{
   154→			name: "pre-push",
   155→			content: fmt.Sprintf(`#!/bin/sh
   156→# %s
   157→# Pre-push hook: push session logs alongside user's push
   158→# $1 is the remote name (e.g., "origin")
   159→%s hooks git pre-push "$1" || true
   160→`, entireHookMarker, cmdPrefix),
   161→		},
   162→	}
   163→}
   164→
   165→// InstallGitHook installs generic git hooks that delegate to `entire hook` commands.
   166→// These hooks work with any strategy - the strategy is determined at runtime.
   167→// If silent is true, no output is printed (except backup notifications, which always print).
   168→// Returns the number of hooks that were installed (0 if all already up to date).
   169→func InstallGitHook(silent bool) (int, error) {
   170→	hooksDir, err := GetHooksDir()
   171→	if err != nil {
   172→		return 0, err
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	if err := os.MkdirAll(hooksDir, 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // Git hooks require executable permissions
   176→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create hooks directory: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	specs := buildHookSpecs(hookCmdPrefix())
   180→	installedCount := 0
   181→
   182→	for _, spec := range specs {
   183→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, spec.name)
   184→		backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
   185→		backupExists := fileExists(backupPath)
   186→
   187→		// Back up existing non-Entire hooks
   188→		existing, existingErr := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   189→		if existingErr == nil && !strings.Contains(string(existing), entireHookMarker) {
   190→			if !backupExists {
   191→				if err := os.Rename(hookPath, backupPath); err != nil {
   192→					return installedCount, fmt.Errorf("failed to back up %s: %w", spec.name, err)
   193→				}
   194→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Backed up existing %s to %s%s\n", spec.name, spec.name, backupSuffix)
   195→			} else {
   196→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: replacing %s (backup %s%s already exists from a previous install)\n", spec.name, spec.name, backupSuffix)
   197→			}
   198→			backupExists = true
   199→		}
   200→
   201→		// Chain to backup if one exists
   202→		content := spec.content
   203→		if backupExists {
   204→			content = generateChainedContent(spec.content, spec.name)
   205→		}
   206→
   207→		written, err := writeHookFile(hookPath, content)
   208→		if err != nil {
   209→			return installedCount, fmt.Errorf("failed to install %s hook: %w", spec.name, err)
   210→		}
   211→		if written {
   212→			installedCount++
   213→		}
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	if !silent {
   217→		fmt.Println("✓ Installed git hooks (prepare-commit-msg, commit-msg, post-commit, pre-push)")
   218→		fmt.Println("  Hooks delegate to the current strategy at runtime")
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	return installedCount, nil
   222→}
   223→
   224→// writeHookFile writes a hook file if it doesn't exist or has different content.
   225→// Returns true if the file was written, false if it already had the same content.
   226→func writeHookFile(path, content string) (bool, error) {
   227→	// Check if file already exists with same content
   228→	existing, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   229→	if err == nil && string(existing) == content {
   230→		return false, nil // Already up to date
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Git hooks must be executable (0o755)
   234→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), 0o755); err != nil { //nolint:gosec // Git hooks require executable permissions
   235→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to write hook file %s: %w", path, err)
   236→	}
   237→	return true, nil
   238→}
   239→
   240→// RemoveGitHook removes all Entire CLI git hooks from the repository.
   241→// If a .pre-entire backup exists, it is restored.
   242→// Returns the number of hooks removed.
   243→func RemoveGitHook() (int, error) {
   244→	hooksDir, err := GetHooksDir()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return 0, err
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	removed := 0
   250→	var removeErrors []string
   251→
   252→	for _, hook := range gitHookNames {
   253→		hookPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, hook)
   254→		backupPath := hookPath + backupSuffix
   255→
   256→		// Remove the hook if it contains our marker
   257→		data, err := os.ReadFile(hookPath) //nolint:gosec // path is controlled
   258→		hookIsOurs := err == nil && strings.Contains(string(data), entireHookMarker)
   259→		hookExists := err == nil
   260→
   261→		if hookIsOurs {
   262→			if err := os.Remove(hookPath); err != nil {
   263→				removeErrors = append(removeErrors, fmt.Sprintf("%s: %v", hook, err))
   264→				continue
   265→			}
   266→			removed++
   267→		}
   268→
   269→		// Restore .pre-entire backup if it exists
   270→		if fileExists(backupPath) {
   271→			if hookExists && !hookIsOurs {
   272→				// A non-Entire hook is present — don't overwrite it with the backup
   273→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: %s was modified since install; backup %s%s left in place\n", hook, hook, backupSuffix)
   274→			} else {
   275→				if err := os.Rename(backupPath, hookPath); err != nil {
   276→					removeErrors = append(removeErrors, fmt.Sprintf("restore %s%s: %v", hook, backupSuffix, err))
   277→				}
   278→			}
   279→		}
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	if len(removeErrors) > 0 {
   283→		return removed, fmt.Errorf("failed to remove hooks: %s", strings.Join(removeErrors, "; "))
   284→	}
   285→	return removed, nil
   286→}
   287→
   288→// generateChainedContent appends a chain call to the base hook content,
   289→// so the pre-existing hook (backed up to .pre-entire) is called after our hook.
   290→func generateChainedContent(baseContent, hookName string) string {
   291→	return baseContent + fmt.Sprintf(`%s
   292→_entire_hook_dir="$(dirname "$0")"
   293→if [ -x "$_entire_hook_dir/%s%s" ]; then
   294→    "$_entire_hook_dir/%s%s" "$@"
   295→fi
   296→`, chainComment, hookName, backupSuffix, hookName, backupSuffix)
   297→}
   298→
   299→// hookCmdPrefix returns the command prefix for hook scripts and warning messages.
   300→// Returns "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go" when local_dev is enabled, "entire" otherwise.
   301→func hookCmdPrefix() string {
   302→	if isLocalDev() {
   303→		return "go run ./cmd/entire/main.go"
   304→	}
   305→	return "entire"
   306→}
   307→
   308→// isLocalDev reads the local_dev setting from .entire/settings.json
   309→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
   310→func isLocalDev() bool {
   311→	s, err := settings.Load()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return false
   314→	}
   315→	return s.LocalDev
   316→}
   317→

    16→// gitHookContext holds common state for git hook logging.
    17→type gitHookContext struct {

    19→	ctx          context.Context
    20→	start        time.Time
    21→	strategy     strategy.Strategy

    24→
    25→// newGitHookContext creates a new git hook context with logging initialized.
    26→func newGitHookContext(hookName string) *gitHookContext {
    27→	g := &gitHookContext{
    28→		hookName:     hookName,
    29→		start:        time.Now(),
    30→		ctx:          logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"),
    31→		strategyName: unknownStrategyName,
    32→	}
    33→	g.strategy = GetStrategy()
    34→	g.strategyName = g.strategy.Name()
    35→	return g
    36→}
    37→
    38→// logInvoked logs that the hook was invoked.
    39→func (g *gitHookContext) logInvoked(extraAttrs ...any) {
    40→	attrs := []any{
    41→		slog.String("hook", g.hookName),
    42→		slog.String("hook_type", "git"),
    43→		slog.String("strategy", g.strategyName),
    44→	}
    45→	logging.Debug(g.ctx, g.hookName+" hook invoked", append(attrs, extraAttrs...)...)
    46→}
    47→
    48→// logCompleted logs hook completion with duration at DEBUG level.
    49→// The actual work logging (checkpoint operations) happens at INFO level in the handlers.
    50→func (g *gitHookContext) logCompleted(err error, extraAttrs ...any) {
    51→	attrs := []any{
    52→		slog.String("hook", g.hookName),
    53→		slog.String("hook_type", "git"),
    54→		slog.String("strategy", g.strategyName),
    55→		slog.Bool("success", err == nil),
    56→	}
    57→	logging.LogDuration(g.ctx, slog.LevelDebug, g.hookName+" hook completed", g.start, append(attrs, extraAttrs...)...)
    58→}

    60→// initHookLogging initializes logging for hooks by finding the most recent session.
    61→// Returns a cleanup function that should be deferred.
    62→func initHookLogging() func() {
    63→	// Set up log level getter so logging can read from settings
    64→	logging.SetLogLevelGetter(GetLogLevel)
    65→
    66→	// Read session ID for the slog attribute (empty string is fine - log file is fixed)
    67→	sessionID := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
    68→	if err := logging.Init(sessionID); err != nil {
    69→		// Init failed - logging will use stderr fallback
    70→		return func() {}
    71→	}
    72→	return logging.Close
    73→}

    30→
    31→// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
    32→// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
    33→//
    34→// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
    35→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
    36→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
    37→func hasTTY() bool {
    38→	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
    39→		return v == "1"
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands.
    43→	// Gemini subprocesses may have access to the user's TTY, but they can't
    44→	// actually respond to interactive prompts. Treat them as non-TTY.
    45→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    46→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    47→		return false
    48→	}
    49→
    50→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return false
    53→	}
    54→	_ = tty.Close()
    55→	return true
    56→}
    57→
    58→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    59→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    60→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    61→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    62→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    63→	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
    64→	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
    65→	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
    66→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
    67→		return defaultYes
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands (including git commit).
    71→	// The agent can't respond to TTY prompts, so use the default to avoid hanging.
    72→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    73→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    74→		return defaultYes
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    78→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    79→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    80→	if err != nil {
    81→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    82→		return defaultYes
    83→	}
    84→	defer tty.Close()
    85→
    86→	// Show context if provided
    87→	if context != "" {
    88→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    92→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    93→	var hint string
    94→	if defaultYes {
    95→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    96→	} else {
    97→		hint = "[y/N]"
    98→	}
    99→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
   100→
   101→	// Read response
   102→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
   103→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
   104→	if err != nil {
   105→		return defaultYes
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
   109→	switch response {
   110→	case "y", "yes":
   111→		return true
   112→	case "n", "no":
   113→		return false
   114→	default:
   115→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
   116→		return defaultYes
   117→	}
   118→}
   119→
   120→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   121→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
   122→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
   123→//
   124→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   125→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
   126→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	message := string(content)
   132→
   133→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   134→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
   135→		// No trailer, nothing to do
   136→		return nil
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
   140→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
   141→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
   142→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
   143→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   144→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	return nil
   149→}
   150→
   151→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   152→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   153→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   154→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   155→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   156→		// Skip empty lines
   157→		if trimmed == "" {
   158→			continue
   159→		}
   160→		// Skip comment lines
   161→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   162→			continue
   163→		}
   164→		// Skip our trailer line
   165→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   166→			continue
   167→		}
   168→		// Found user content
   169→		return true
   170→	}
   171→	return false
   172→}
   173→
   174→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   175→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   176→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   177→	var result []string
   178→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   179→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   180→			result = append(result, line)
   181→		}
   182→	}
   183→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   184→}
   185→
   186→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   187→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   188→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   189→//
   190→// Detects:
   191→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   192→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   193→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   194→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   195→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   196→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Check for rebase state directories
   202→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   203→		return true
   204→	}
   205→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   206→		return true
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   210→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   211→		return true
   212→	}
   213→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   214→		return true
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	return false
   218→}
   219→
   220→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   221→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   222→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   223→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   224→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   225→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   226→//
   227→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   228→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   229→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   230→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   231→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from LastCheckpointID
   232→//
   233→
   234→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   235→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   236→
   237→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   238→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   239→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   240→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   241→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   242→			slog.String("source", source),
   243→		)
   244→		return nil
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   248→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   249→	switch source {
   250→	case "merge", "squash":
   251→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   252→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   253→			slog.String("source", source),
   254→		)
   255→		return nil
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   259→	if source == "commit" {
   260→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   269→	if err != nil {
   270→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   274→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   275→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   276→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   277→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   278→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   279→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   280→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   281→			slog.String("source", source),
   282→		)
   283→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
   287→	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
   288→	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
   289→	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
   290→	// add the trailer directly.
   291→	if !hasTTY() {
   292→		for _, state := range sessions {
   293→			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   294→				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
   295→			}
   296→		}
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   300→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   301→
   302→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   303→		// No new content — no trailer needed
   304→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   305→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   306→			slog.String("source", source),
   307→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   308→		)
   309→		return nil
   310→	}
   311→
   312→	// Read current commit message
   313→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	message := string(content)
   319→
   320→	// Check if trailer already exists (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   321→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   322→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   323→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   324→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   325→			slog.String("source", source),
   326→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   327→		)
   328→		return nil
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	// Generate a fresh checkpoint ID
   332→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   333→	if err != nil {
   334→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   338→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   339→	var lastPrompt string
   340→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   341→		firstSession := sessionsWithContent[0]
   342→		if firstSession.AgentType != "" {
   343→			agentType = firstSession.AgentType
   344→		}
   345→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, firstSession)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   349→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   350→
   351→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   352→	switch source {
   353→	case "message":
   354→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   355→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   356→
   357→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   358→		var promptContext string
   359→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   360→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   361→		}
   362→
   363→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   364→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   365→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   366→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   367→				slog.String("source", source),
   368→			)
   369→			return nil
   370→		}
   371→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   372→	default:
   373→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   374→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   375→	}
   376→
   377→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   378→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   379→		slog.String("source", source),
   380→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   381→	)
   382→
   383→	// Write updated message back
   384→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   385→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	return nil
   389→}
   390→
   391→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   392→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from LastCheckpointID.
   393→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   394→	// Read current commit message
   395→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	message := string(content)
   401→
   402→	// If message already has a trailer, keep it unchanged
   403→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   404→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend preserves existing trailer",
   405→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   406→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   407→		)
   408→		return nil
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	// No trailer in message — check if any session has LastCheckpointID to restore
   412→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   418→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   419→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions - nothing to restore
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	// For amend, HEAD^ is the commit being amended, and HEAD is where we are now.
   423→	// We need to match sessions whose BaseCommit equals HEAD (the commit being amended
   424→	// was created from this base). This prevents stale sessions from injecting
   425→	// unrelated checkpoint IDs.
   426→	repo, repoErr := OpenRepository()
   427→	if repoErr != nil {
   428→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   429→	}
   430→	head, headErr := repo.Head()
   431→	if headErr != nil {
   432→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   433→	}
   434→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   435→
   436→	// Find first matching session with LastCheckpointID to restore.
   437→	// LastCheckpointID is set after condensation completes.
   438→	for _, state := range sessions {
   439→		if state.BaseCommit != currentHead {
   440→			continue
   441→		}
   442→		if state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   443→			continue
   444→		}
   445→		cpID := state.LastCheckpointID
   446→		source := "LastCheckpointID"
   447→
   448→		// Restore the trailer
   449→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
   450→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   451→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   452→		}
   453→
   454→		logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: restored trailer on amend",
   455→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   456→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   457→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   458→			slog.String("source", source),
   459→		)
   460→		return nil
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	// No checkpoint ID found - leave message unchanged
   464→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend with no checkpoint to restore",
   465→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   466→	)
   467→	return nil
   468→}
   469→
   470→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   471→// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:
   472→//   - ACTIVE → condense immediately (each commit gets its own checkpoint)
   473→//   - IDLE → condense immediately
   474→//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty
   475→//
   476→// After condensation for ACTIVE sessions, remaining uncommitted files are
   477→// carried forward to a new shadow branch so the next commit gets its own checkpoint.
   478→//
   479→// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active
   480→// and were condensed during this PostCommit.
   481→
   482→// postCommitActionHandler implements session.ActionHandler for PostCommit.
   483→// Each session in the loop gets its own handler with per-session context.
   484→// Handler methods use the *State parameter from ApplyTransition (same pointer
   485→// as the state being transitioned) rather than capturing state separately.
   486→type postCommitActionHandler struct {
   487→	s                      *ManualCommitStrategy
   488→	logCtx                 context.Context
   489→	repo                   *git.Repository
   490→	checkpointID           id.CheckpointID
   491→	head                   *plumbing.Reference
   492→	commit                 *object.Commit
   493→	newHead                string
   494→	shadowBranchName       string
   495→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{}
   496→	committedFileSet       map[string]struct{}
   497→	hasNew                 bool
   498→	filesTouchedBefore     []string
   499→
   500→	// Output: set by handler methods, read by caller after TransitionAndLog.
   501→	condensed bool
   502→}
   503→
   504→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {
   505→	shouldCondense := h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   506→
   507→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondense decision",
   508→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   509→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   510→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   511→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   512→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   513→	)
   514→
   515→	if shouldCondense {
   516→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   517→	} else {
   518→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   519→	}
   520→	return nil
   521→}
   522→
   523→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
   524→	shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   525→
   526→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched decision",
   527→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   528→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   529→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   530→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   531→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   532→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   533→	)
   534→
   535→	if shouldCondense {
   536→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   537→	} else {
   538→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   539→	}
   540→	return nil
   541→}
   542→
   543→// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed
   544→// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files
   545→// overlap with the committed files with matching content.
   546→//
   547→// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or
   548→// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every
   549→// unrelated commit.
   550→//
   551→// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
   552→//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set via SaveStep/SaveTaskStep -> mergeFilesTouched)
   553→//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions when FilesTouched is empty
   554→//
   555→// When filesTouchedBefore is empty:
   556→//   - For ACTIVE sessions: fail-open (trust hasNew) because the agent may be
   557→//     mid-turn before any files are saved to state.
   558→//   - For IDLE/ENDED sessions: return false because there are no files to
   559→//     overlap with the commit.
   560→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(isActive bool) bool {
   561→	if !h.hasNew {
   562→		return false
   563→	}
   564→	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
   565→		return isActive // ACTIVE: fail-open; IDLE/ENDED: no files = no overlap
   566→	}
   567→	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
   568→	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
   569→	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
   570→	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
   571→	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
   572→	var committedTouchedFiles []string
   573→	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
   574→		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
   575→			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
   576→		}
   577→	}
   578→	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
   579→		return false
   580→	}
   581→	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
   582→}
   583→
   584→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *session.State) error {
   585→	if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   586→		logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   587→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   588→		)
   589→	}
   590→	h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   591→	return nil
   592→}
   593→
   594→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleWarnStaleSession(_ *session.State) error {
   595→	// Not produced by EventGitCommit; no-op for exhaustiveness.
   596→	return nil
   597→}
   598→
   599→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   600→//
   601→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   602→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   603→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   604→
   605→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   606→	if err != nil {
   607→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   608→	}
   609→
   610→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   611→	head, err := repo.Head()
   612→	if err != nil {
   613→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   617→	if err != nil {
   618→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   622→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   623→	if !found {
   624→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   625→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   626→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   627→		return nil
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   631→	if err != nil {
   632→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   636→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   637→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   638→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   639→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   640→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   641→		)
   642→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Build transition context
   646→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   647→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   648→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   649→	}
   650→
   651→	if isRebase {
   652→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   653→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   654→		)
   655→	}
   656→
   657→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   658→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   659→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   660→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   661→
   662→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   663→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   664→
   665→	for _, state := range sessions {
   666→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   667→
   668→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   669→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   670→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   671→		//
   672→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   673→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   674→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   675→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   676→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   677→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   678→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   679→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   680→		var hasNew bool
   681→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   682→			hasNew = true
   683→		} else {
   684→			var contentErr error
   685→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   686→			if contentErr != nil {
   687→				hasNew = true
   688→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   689→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   690→					slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   691→				)
   692→			}
   693→		}
   694→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   695→
   696→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   697→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   698→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   699→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   700→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   701→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   702→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   703→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   704→		}
   705→
   706→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward prep",
   707→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   708→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   709→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   710→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   711→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),
   712→		)
   713→
   714→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   715→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{
   716→			s:                      s,
   717→			logCtx:                 logCtx,
   718→			repo:                   repo,
   719→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,
   720→			head:                   head,
   721→			commit:                 commit,
   722→			newHead:                newHead,
   723→			shadowBranchName:       shadowBranchName,
   724→			shadowBranchesToDelete: shadowBranchesToDelete,
   725→			committedFileSet:       committedFileSet,
   726→			hasNew:                 hasNew,
   727→			filesTouchedBefore:     filesTouchedBefore,
   728→		}
   729→
   730→		if err := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx, handler); err != nil {
   731→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   732→		}
   733→
   734→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   735→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   736→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   737→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   738→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   739→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   740→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   741→		}
   742→
   743→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   744→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   745→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   746→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   747→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   748→		if handler.condensed {
   749→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   750→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   751→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   752→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   753→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   754→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   755→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   756→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   757→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   758→			)
   759→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   760→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   761→			}
   762→		}
   763→
   764→		// Save the updated state
   765→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   766→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   767→		}
   768→
   769→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   770→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   771→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   772→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   773→		}
   774→	}
   775→
   776→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   777→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   778→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   779→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   780→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   781→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   782→			)
   783→			continue
   784→		}
   785→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   786→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   787→		} else {
   788→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   789→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   790→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   791→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   792→			)
   793→		}
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	return nil
   797→}
   798→
   799→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   800→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   801→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   802→	logCtx context.Context,
   803→	repo *git.Repository,
   804→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   805→	state *SessionState,
   806→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   807→	shadowBranchName string,
   808→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   809→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   810→) bool {
   811→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   812→	if err != nil {
   813→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   814→			state.SessionID, err)
   815→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   816→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   817→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   818→		)
   819→		return false
   820→	}
   821→
   822→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   823→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   824→
   825→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   826→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   827→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   828→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   829→	state.StepCount = 0
   830→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   831→
   832→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   833→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   834→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   835→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   836→
   837→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   838→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   839→
   840→	shortID := state.SessionID
   841→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   842→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   843→	}
   844→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   845→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   846→
   847→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   848→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   849→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   850→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   851→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   852→	)
   853→
   854→	return true
   855→}
   856→
   857→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   858→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   859→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   860→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   861→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   862→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   863→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   864→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   865→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   866→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   867→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   868→		)
   869→		return
   870→	}
   871→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   872→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   873→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   874→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   875→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   876→		)
   877→	}
   878→}
   879→
   880→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   881→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   882→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   883→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   884→//
   885→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   886→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   887→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   888→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   889→	if err != nil {
   890→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   891→	}
   892→
   893→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   894→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   895→		return
   896→	}
   897→
   898→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   899→	for _, state := range sessions {
   900→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   901→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   902→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   903→			continue
   904→		}
