     1→     1→//go:build e2e
     2→     2→
     3→     3→package e2e
     4→     4→
     5→     5→import (
     6→     6→	"fmt"
     7→     7→	"testing"
     8→     8→
     9→     9→	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
    10→    10→	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"
    11→    11→)
    12→    12→
    13→    13→// These tests cover the scenarios documented in docs/architecture/checkpoint-scenarios.md
    14→    14→
    15→    15→// TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits tests Claude making multiple granular commits
    16→    16→// during a single turn.
    17→    17→//
    18→    18→// Scenario 3: Claude Makes Multiple Granular Commits
    19→    19→// - Claude is instructed to make granular commits
    20→    20→// - Multiple commits happen during one turn
    21→    21→// - Each commit gets its own unique checkpoint ID
    22→    22→// - All checkpoints are finalized together at turn end
    23→    23→func TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits(t *testing.T) {
    24→    24→	t.Parallel()
    25→    25→
    26→    26→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
    27→    27→
    28→    28→	// Count commits before
    29→    29→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
    30→    30→	t.Logf("Commits before: %d", commitsBefore)
    31→    31→
    32→    32→	// Agent creates multiple files and commits them separately
    33→    33→	granularCommitPrompt := `Please do the following tasks, committing after each one:
    34→    34→
    35→    35→1. Create a file called file1.go with this content:
    36→    36→   package main
    37→    37→   func One() int { return 1 }
    38→    38→   Then run: git add file1.go && git commit -m "Add file1"
    39→    39→
    40→    40→2. Create a file called file2.go with this content:
    41→    41→   package main
    42→    42→   func Two() int { return 2 }
    43→    43→   Then run: git add file2.go && git commit -m "Add file2"
    44→    44→
    45→    45→3. Create a file called file3.go with this content:
    46→    46→   package main
    47→    47→   func Three() int { return 3 }
    48→    48→   Then run: git add file3.go && git commit -m "Add file3"
    49→    49→
    50→    50→Do each task in order, making the commit after each file creation.`
    51→    51→
    52→    52→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(granularCommitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
    53→    53→	require.NoError(t, err)
    54→    54→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
    55→    55→	t.Logf("Agent output: %s", result.Stdout)
    56→    56→
    57→    57→	// Verify all files were created
    58→    58→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file1.go"), "file1.go should exist")
    59→    59→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file2.go"), "file2.go should exist")
    60→    60→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("file3.go"), "file3.go should exist")
    61→    61→
    62→    62→	// Verify multiple commits were made
    63→    63→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
    64→    64→	t.Logf("Commits after: %d", commitsAfter)
    65→    65→	assert.GreaterOrEqual(t, commitsAfter-commitsBefore, 3, "Should have at least 3 new commits")
    66→    66→
    67→    67→	// Get all checkpoint IDs from history
    68→    68→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
    69→    69→	t.Logf("Found %d checkpoint IDs in commit history", len(checkpointIDs))
    70→    70→	for i, id := range checkpointIDs {
    71→    71→		t.Logf("  Checkpoint %d: %s", i, id)
    72→    72→	}
    73→    73→
    74→    74→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID (1:1 model)
    75→    75→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 3 {
    76→    76→		// Verify checkpoint IDs are unique
    77→    77→		idSet := make(map[string]bool)
    78→    78→		for _, id := range checkpointIDs {
    79→    79→			require.Falsef(t, idSet[id], "Checkpoint IDs should be unique: %s is duplicated", id)
    80→    80→			idSet[id] = true
    81→    81→		}
    82→    82→	}
    83→    83→
    84→    84→	// Verify metadata branch exists
    85→    85→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
    86→    86→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
    87→    87→
    88→    88→	// Validate each checkpoint has proper metadata and content
    89→    89→	// Note: checkpointIDs are in reverse chronological order (newest first)
    90→    90→	// So checkpointIDs[0] = file3.go, [1] = file2.go, [2] = file1.go
    91→    91→	//
    92→    92→	// With deferred finalization, all checkpoints from the same turn get the
    93→    93→	// FULL transcript at turn end, so all checkpoints should contain all file names.
    94→    94→	allFiles := []string{"file1.go", "file2.go", "file3.go"}
    95→    95→	for i, cpID := range checkpointIDs {
    96→    96→		fileNum := len(checkpointIDs) - i // Reverse the index to match file numbers
    97→    97→		fileName := fmt.Sprintf("file%d.go", fileNum)
    98→    98→		t.Logf("Validating checkpoint %d: %s (files_touched: %s)", i, cpID, fileName)
    99→    99→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   100→   100→			CheckpointID:              cpID,
   101→   101→			Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   102→   102→			FilesTouched:              []string{fileName},
   103→   103→			ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // All checkpoints have full transcript
   104→   104→		})
   105→   105→	}
   106→   106→
   107→   107→	// No shadow branches should remain after agent committed all files
   108→   108→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   109→   109→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   110→   110→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   111→   111→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   112→   112→	}
   113→   113→}
   114→   114→
   115→   115→// TestE2E_Scenario4_UserSplitsCommits tests user splitting agent changes into multiple commits.
   116→   116→//
   117→   117→// Scenario 4: User Splits Changes Into Multiple Commits
   118→   118→// - Agent makes changes to multiple files
   119→   119→// - User commits only some files first
   120→   120→// - Uncommitted files are carried forward
   121→   121→// - User commits remaining files later
   122→   122→// - Each commit gets its own checkpoint ID
   123→   123→func TestE2E_Scenario4_UserSplitsCommits(t *testing.T) {
   124→   124→	t.Parallel()
   125→   125→
   126→   126→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   127→   127→
   128→   128→	// Agent creates multiple files in one prompt
   129→   129→	multiFilePrompt := `Create these files:
   130→   130→1. fileA.go with content: package main; func A() string { return "A" }
   131→   131→2. fileB.go with content: package main; func B() string { return "B" }
   132→   132→3. fileC.go with content: package main; func C() string { return "C" }
   133→   133→4. fileD.go with content: package main; func D() string { return "D" }
   134→   134→
   135→   135→Create all four files, no other files or actions.`
   136→   136→
   137→   137→	result, err := env.RunAgent(multiFilePrompt)
   138→   138→	require.NoError(t, err)
   139→   139→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   140→   140→
   141→   141→	// Verify all files were created
   142→   142→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileA.go"), "fileA.go should exist")
   143→   143→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileB.go"), "fileB.go should exist")
   144→   144→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileC.go"), "fileC.go should exist")
   145→   145→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("fileD.go"), "fileD.go should exist")
   146→   146→
   147→   147→	// Check rewind points before commit
   148→   148→	pointsBefore := env.GetRewindPoints()
   149→   149→	t.Logf("Rewind points before commit: %d", len(pointsBefore))
   150→   150→
   151→   151→	// User commits only A, B first
   152→   152→	t.Log("Committing fileA.go and fileB.go only")
   153→   153→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add files A and B", "fileA.go", "fileB.go")
   154→   154→
   155→   155→	// Verify first checkpoint was created
   156→   156→	checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   157→   157→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
   158→   158→	// Note: GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory returns IDs in reverse chronological order (newest first)
   159→   159→	checkpointAB := checkpointIDsAfterFirstCommit[0]
   160→   160→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for A,B commit: %s", checkpointAB)
   161→   161→
   162→   162→	// Check rewind points - should still have points for uncommitted files
   163→   163→	pointsAfterFirst := env.GetRewindPoints()
   164→   164→	t.Logf("Rewind points after first commit: %d", len(pointsAfterFirst))
   165→   165→
   166→   166→	// User commits C, D later
   167→   167→	t.Log("Committing fileC.go and fileD.go")
   168→   168→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add files C and D", "fileC.go", "fileD.go")
   169→   169→
   170→   170→	// Verify second checkpoint was created with different ID
   171→   171→	checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   172→   172→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
   173→   173→	checkpointCD := checkpointIDsAfterSecondCommit[0] // Most recent (C,D commit) is first
   174→   174→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for C,D commit: %s", checkpointCD)
   175→   175→
   176→   176→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID (1:1 model)
   177→   177→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointAB, checkpointCD,
   178→   178→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
   179→   179→
   180→   180→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   181→   181→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   182→   182→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   183→   183→
   184→   184→	// Both checkpoints are from the same session where agent created all 4 files.
   185→   185→	// The transcript should contain all file names since it's the same agent work.
   186→   186→	allFiles := []string{"fileA.go", "fileB.go", "fileC.go", "fileD.go"}
   187→   187→
   188→   188→	// Validate first checkpoint (files A, B committed)
   189→   189→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   190→   190→		CheckpointID:              checkpointAB,
   191→   191→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   192→   192→		FilesTouched:              []string{"fileA.go", "fileB.go"},
   193→   193→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // Full session transcript
   194→   194→	})
   195→   195→
   196→   196→	// Validate second checkpoint (files C, D committed)
   197→   197→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   198→   198→		CheckpointID:              checkpointCD,
   199→   199→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   200→   200→		FilesTouched:              []string{"fileC.go", "fileD.go"},
   201→   201→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: allFiles, // Full session transcript
   202→   202→	})
   203→   203→
   204→   204→	// CRITICAL: After all carry-forward files are committed, no shadow branches
   205→   205→	// should remain. Only entire/checkpoints/v1 should exist. Extra shadow
   206→   206→	// branches (entire/<hash>-<hash>) indicate a regression in carry-forward cleanup.
   207→   207→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   208→   208→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   209→   209→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   210→   210→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   211→   211→	}
   212→   212→	t.Logf("Entire branches after all commits: %v", entireBranches)
   213→   213→}
   214→   214→
   215→   215→// TestE2E_Scenario5_PartialCommitStashNextPrompt tests partial commit, stash, then new prompt.
   216→   216→//
   217→   217→// Scenario 5: Partial Commit → Stash → Next Prompt
   218→   218→// - Agent makes changes to A, B, C
   219→   219→// - User commits A only
   220→   220→// - User stashes B, C
   221→   221→// - User runs another prompt (creates D, E)
   222→   222→// - User commits D, E
   223→   223→// - FilesTouched accumulates across prompts
   224→   224→func TestE2E_Scenario5_PartialCommitStashNextPrompt(t *testing.T) {
   225→   225→	t.Parallel()
   226→   226→
   227→   227→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   228→   228→
   229→   229→	// Prompt 1: Agent creates files A, B, C
   230→   230→	t.Log("Prompt 1: Creating files A, B, C")
   231→   231→	prompt1 := `Create these files:
   232→   232→1. stash_a.go with content: package main; func StashA() {}
   233→   233→2. stash_b.go with content: package main; func StashB() {}
   234→   234→3. stash_c.go with content: package main; func StashC() {}
   235→   235→Create all three files, nothing else.`
   236→   236→
   237→   237→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt1)
   238→   238→	require.NoError(t, err)
   239→   239→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   240→   240→
   241→   241→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_a.go"))
   242→   242→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_b.go"))
   243→   243→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_c.go"))
   244→   244→
   245→   245→	// User commits A only
   246→   246→	t.Log("Committing stash_a.go only")
   247→   247→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add stash_a", "stash_a.go")
   248→   248→
   249→   249→	// User stashes B, C
   250→   250→	t.Log("Stashing remaining files")
   251→   251→	env.GitStash()
   252→   252→
   253→   253→	// Verify B, C are no longer in working directory
   254→   254→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("stash_b.go"), "stash_b.go should be stashed")
   255→   255→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("stash_c.go"), "stash_c.go should be stashed")
   256→   256→
   257→   257→	// Prompt 2: Agent creates files D, E
   258→   258→	t.Log("Prompt 2: Creating files D, E")
   259→   259→	prompt2 := `Create these files:
   260→   260→1. stash_d.go with content: package main; func StashD() {}
   261→   261→2. stash_e.go with content: package main; func StashE() {}
   262→   262→Create both files, nothing else.`
   263→   263→
   264→   264→	result, err = env.RunAgent(prompt2)
   265→   265→	require.NoError(t, err)
   266→   266→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   267→   267→
   268→   268→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_d.go"))
   269→   269→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("stash_e.go"))
   270→   270→
   271→   271→	// User commits D, E
   272→   272→	t.Log("Committing stash_d.go and stash_e.go")
   273→   273→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add stash_d and stash_e", "stash_d.go", "stash_e.go")
   274→   274→
   275→   275→	// Verify checkpoint was created for D, E
   276→   276→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   277→   277→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 2, "Should have checkpoints for both commits")
   278→   278→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   279→   279→
   280→   280→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   281→   281→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   282→   282→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   283→   283→
   284→   284→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata
   285→   285→	// checkpointIDs[0] is the most recent (D, E commit from prompt 2)
   286→   286→	// checkpointIDs[1] is the earlier commit (A only from prompt 1)
   287→   287→	//
   288→   288→	// Note: We don't validate transcript content here because:
   289→   289→	// 1. Claude's response text varies and may not contain exact file names
   290→   290→	// 2. Multi-session checkpoints have multiple session folders, making validation complex
   291→   291→	// The key validation is that files_touched is correct for each checkpoint.
   292→   292→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 2 {
   293→   293→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   294→   294→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[0],
   295→   295→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   296→   296→			FilesTouched: []string{"stash_d.go", "stash_e.go"},
   297→   297→		})
   298→   298→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   299→   299→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[1],
   300→   300→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   301→   301→			FilesTouched: []string{"stash_a.go"},
   302→   302→		})
   303→   303→	}
   304→   304→
   305→   305→	// Note: stash_b.go and stash_c.go are still stashed (never committed),
   306→   306→	// so a shadow branch may remain for the session that still has pending files.
   307→   307→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   308→   308→	t.Logf("Entire branches after commits: %v", entireBranches)
   309→   309→}
   310→   310→
   311→   311→// TestE2E_Scenario6_StashSecondPromptUnstashCommitAll tests stash, new prompt, unstash, commit all.
   312→   312→//
   313→   313→// Scenario 6: Stash → Second Prompt → Unstash → Commit All
   314→   314→// - Agent makes changes to A, B, C
   315→   315→// - User commits A only
   316→   316→// - User stashes B, C
   317→   317→// - User runs another prompt (creates D, E)
   318→   318→// - User unstashes B, C
   319→   319→// - User commits ALL (B, C, D, E) together
   320→   320→// - All files link to single checkpoint
   321→   321→func TestE2E_Scenario6_StashSecondPromptUnstashCommitAll(t *testing.T) {
   322→   322→	t.Parallel()
   323→   323→
   324→   324→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   325→   325→
   326→   326→	// Prompt 1: Agent creates files A, B, C
   327→   327→	t.Log("Prompt 1: Creating files A, B, C")
   328→   328→	prompt1 := `Create these files:
   329→   329→1. combo_a.go with content: package main; func ComboA() {}
   330→   330→2. combo_b.go with content: package main; func ComboB() {}
   331→   331→3. combo_c.go with content: package main; func ComboC() {}
   332→   332→Create all three files, nothing else.`
   333→   333→
   334→   334→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt1)
   335→   335→	require.NoError(t, err)
   336→   336→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   337→   337→
   338→   338→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_a.go"))
   339→   339→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"))
   340→   340→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"))
   341→   341→
   342→   342→	// User commits A only
   343→   343→	t.Log("Committing combo_a.go only")
   344→   344→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add combo_a", "combo_a.go")
   345→   345→
   346→   346→	// User stashes B, C
   347→   347→	t.Log("Stashing remaining files B, C")
   348→   348→	env.GitStash()
   349→   349→
   350→   350→	// Verify B, C are no longer in working directory
   351→   351→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"), "combo_b.go should be stashed")
   352→   352→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"), "combo_c.go should be stashed")
   353→   353→
   354→   354→	// Prompt 2: Agent creates files D, E
   355→   355→	t.Log("Prompt 2: Creating files D, E")
   356→   356→	prompt2 := `Create these files:
   357→   357→1. combo_d.go with content: package main; func ComboD() {}
   358→   358→2. combo_e.go with content: package main; func ComboE() {}
   359→   359→Create both files, nothing else.`
   360→   360→
   361→   361→	result, err = env.RunAgent(prompt2)
   362→   362→	require.NoError(t, err)
   363→   363→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   364→   364→
   365→   365→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_d.go"))
   366→   366→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_e.go"))
   367→   367→
   368→   368→	// User unstashes B, C
   369→   369→	t.Log("Unstashing B, C")
   370→   370→	env.GitStashPop()
   371→   371→
   372→   372→	// Verify B, C are back
   373→   373→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_b.go"), "combo_b.go should be back after unstash")
   374→   374→	assert.True(t, env.FileExists("combo_c.go"), "combo_c.go should be back after unstash")
   375→   375→
   376→   376→	// User commits ALL files together (B, C, D, E)
   377→   377→	t.Log("Committing all remaining files together")
   378→   378→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add combo_b, combo_c, combo_d, combo_e",
   379→   379→		"combo_b.go", "combo_c.go", "combo_d.go", "combo_e.go")
   380→   380→
   381→   381→	// Verify checkpoint was created
   382→   382→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   383→   383→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 2, "Should have checkpoints")
   384→   384→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   385→   385→
   386→   386→	// The second commit should have all 4 files linked to a single checkpoint
   387→   387→	// Verify metadata branch exists
   388→   388→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   389→   389→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   390→   390→
   391→   391→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata
   392→   392→	// checkpointIDs[0] is the most recent (B, C, D, E combined commit)
   393→   393→	// checkpointIDs[1] is the earlier commit (A only)
   394→   394→	//
   395→   395→	// Prompt 1 created A, B, C. User committed A, then stashed B, C.
   396→   396→	// Prompt 2 created D, E. User unstashed B, C, then committed all 4 together.
   397→   397→	//
   398→   398→	// Note: We don't validate transcript content here because:
   399→   399→	// 1. Claude's response text varies and may not contain exact file names
   400→   400→	// 2. Multi-session checkpoints have multiple session folders, making validation complex
   401→   401→	// The key validation is that files_touched is correct for each checkpoint.
   402→   402→	if len(checkpointIDs) >= 2 {
   403→   403→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   404→   404→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[0],
   405→   405→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   406→   406→			FilesTouched: []string{"combo_b.go", "combo_c.go", "combo_d.go", "combo_e.go"},
   407→   407→		})
   408→   408→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   409→   409→			CheckpointID: checkpointIDs[1],
   410→   410→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   411→   411→			FilesTouched: []string{"combo_a.go"},
   412→   412→		})
   413→   413→	}
   414→   414→
   415→   415→	// No shadow branches should remain after all files committed
   416→   416→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   417→   417→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   418→   418→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   419→   419→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   420→   420→	}
   421→   421→}
   422→   422→
   423→   423→// TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingWithGitAddP tests partial staging with git add -p.
   424→   424→//
   425→   425→// Scenario 7: Partial Staging with `git add -p`
   426→   426→// - Agent makes multiple changes to a single file
   427→   427→// - User stages only part of the changes (simulated with partial write)
   428→   428→// - Content-aware carry-forward detects partial commit
   429→   429→// - Remaining changes carried forward to next commit
   430→   430→func TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingSimulated(t *testing.T) {
   431→   431→	t.Parallel()
   432→   432→
   433→   433→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   434→   434→
   435→   435→	// Create partial.go as an existing tracked file first.
   436→   436→	// For MODIFIED files (vs NEW files), content-aware detection always
   437→   437→	// creates checkpoints regardless of content changes. This allows us
   438→   438→	// to test the partial staging scenario.
   439→   439→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", "package main\n\n// placeholder\n")
   440→   440→	env.GitAdd("partial.go")
   441→   441→	env.GitCommit("Add placeholder partial.go")
   442→   442→
   443→   443→	// Agent modifies the file with multiple functions
   444→   444→	t.Log("Agent modifying file with multiple functions")
   445→   445→	multiLinePrompt := `Replace the contents of partial.go with this exact content:
   446→   446→package main
   447→   447→
   448→   448→func First() int {
   449→   449→	return 1
   450→   450→}
   451→   451→
   452→   452→func Second() int {
   453→   453→	return 2
   454→   454→}
   455→   455→
   456→   456→func Third() int {
   457→   457→	return 3
   458→   458→}
   459→   459→
   460→   460→func Fourth() int {
   461→   461→	return 4
   462→   462→}
   463→   463→
   464→   464→Replace the file with exactly this content, nothing else.`
   465→   465→
   466→   466→	result, err := env.RunAgent(multiLinePrompt)
   467→   467→	require.NoError(t, err)
   468→   468→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   469→   469→
   470→   470→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("partial.go"))
   471→   471→
   472→   472→	// Check rewind points before commit
   473→   473→	pointsBefore := env.GetRewindPoints()
   474→   474→	t.Logf("Rewind points before commit: %d", len(pointsBefore))
   475→   475→
   476→   476→	// Simulate partial staging by temporarily replacing file with partial content
   477→   477→	// Save the full content
   478→   478→	fullContent := env.ReadFile("partial.go")
   479→   479→	t.Logf("Full content length: %d bytes", len(fullContent))
   480→   480→
   481→   481→	// Write partial content (only first two functions)
   482→   482→	partialContent := `package main
   483→   483→
   484→   484→func First() int {
   485→   485→	return 1
   486→   486→}
   487→   487→
   488→   488→func Second() int {
   489→   489→	return 2
   490→   490→}
   491→   491→`
   492→   492→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", partialContent)
   493→   493→
   494→   494→	// Commit the partial content
   495→   495→	t.Log("Committing partial content (First and Second functions)")
   496→   496→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add first two functions", "partial.go")
   497→   497→
   498→   498→	// Verify first checkpoint was created
   499→   499→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   500→   500→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
   501→   501→	t.Logf("First checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointIDs[0])
   502→   502→
   503→   503→	// Restore the full content (simulating remaining changes still in worktree)
   504→   504→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", fullContent)
   505→   505→
   506→   506→	// Commit the remaining content
   507→   507→	t.Log("Committing full content (all functions)")
   508→   508→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add remaining functions", "partial.go")
   509→   509→
   510→   510→	// Verify second checkpoint was created
   511→   511→	checkpointIDsAfter := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   512→   512→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfter), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
   513→   513→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDsAfter)
   514→   514→
   515→   515→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID
   516→   516→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointIDsAfter[0], checkpointIDsAfter[1],
   517→   517→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
   518→   518→
   519→   519→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata and transcripts
   520→   520→	// checkpointIDsAfter[0] is the most recent (full content commit)
   521→   521→	// checkpointIDsAfter[1] is the earlier commit (partial content)
   522→   522→	//
   523→   523→	// Both commits are from the same session (single prompt), so both have
   524→   524→	// the same full transcript referencing partial.go and the function names.
   525→   525→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   526→   526→		CheckpointID:              checkpointIDsAfter[0],
   527→   527→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   528→   528→		FilesTouched:              []string{"partial.go"},
   529→   529→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"partial.go", "First", "Second", "Third", "Fourth"},
   530→   530→	})
   531→   531→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   532→   532→		CheckpointID:              checkpointIDsAfter[1],
   533→   533→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   534→   534→		FilesTouched:              []string{"partial.go"},
   535→   535→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"partial.go", "First", "Second", "Third", "Fourth"},
   536→   536→	})
   537→   537→}
   538→   538→
   539→   539→// TestE2E_ContentAwareOverlap_RevertAndReplace tests content-aware overlap detection
   540→   540→// when user reverts session changes and writes different content.
   541→   541→//
   542→   542→// Content-Aware Overlap Detection:
   543→   543→// - Agent creates file X with content "hello"
   544→   544→// - User reverts X (git checkout -- X)
   545→   545→// - User writes completely different content
   546→   546→// - User commits
   547→   547→// - Content mismatch → NO checkpoint trailer added
   548→   548→func TestE2E_ContentAwareOverlap_RevertAndReplace(t *testing.T) {
   549→   549→	t.Parallel()
   550→   550→
   551→   551→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   552→   552→
   553→   553→	// Agent creates a file
   554→   554→	t.Log("Agent creating file with specific content")
   555→   555→	createPrompt := `Create a file called overlap_test.go with this exact content:
   556→   556→package main
   557→   557→
   558→   558→func OverlapOriginal() string {
   559→   559→	return "original content from agent"
   560→   560→}
   561→   561→
   562→   562→Create only this file.`
   563→   563→
   564→   564→	result, err := env.RunAgent(createPrompt)
   565→   565→	require.NoError(t, err)
   566→   566→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   567→   567→
   568→   568→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("overlap_test.go"))
   569→   569→	originalContent := env.ReadFile("overlap_test.go")
   570→   570→	t.Logf("Original content: %s", originalContent)
   571→   571→
   572→   572→	// Verify rewind points exist (session tracked the change)
   573→   573→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   574→   574→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(points), 1, "Should have rewind points")
   575→   575→
   576→   576→	// User reverts the file and writes completely different content
   577→   577→	t.Log("User reverting file and writing different content")
   578→   578→	differentContent := `package main
   579→   579→
   580→   580→func CompletelyDifferent() string {
   581→   581→	return "user wrote this, not the agent"
   582→   582→}
   583→   583→`
   584→   584→	env.WriteFile("overlap_test.go", differentContent)
   585→   585→
   586→   586→	// Verify content is different
   587→   587→	currentContent := env.ReadFile("overlap_test.go")
   588→   588→	assert.NotEqual(t, originalContent, currentContent)
   589→   589→
   590→   590→	// Commits before this test
   591→   591→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
   592→   592→
   593→   593→	// User commits the different content
   594→   594→	t.Log("Committing user-written content")
   595→   595→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add overlap test file", "overlap_test.go")
   596→   596→
   597→   597→	// Verify commit was made
   598→   598→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
   599→   599→	assert.Equal(t, commitsBefore+1, commitsAfter, "Should have one new commit")
   600→   600→
   601→   601→	// Check for checkpoint trailer
   602→   602→	// With content-aware overlap detection, if the user completely replaced
   603→   603→	// the agent's content (new file with different hash), there should be
   604→   604→	// NO checkpoint trailer added. This is documented in checkpoint-scenarios.md
   605→   605→	// under "Content-Aware Overlap Detection".
   606→   606→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   607→   607→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs found: %v", checkpointIDs)
   608→   608→
   609→   609→	// The first commit (Initial commit from NewFeatureBranchEnv) doesn't have a checkpoint.
   610→   610→	// Only agent-assisted commits should have checkpoint trailers.
   611→   611→	// When user completely replaces agent content, content-aware detection should
   612→   612→	// prevent the checkpoint trailer from being added.
   613→   613→	//
   614→   614→	// Per docs/architecture/checkpoint-scenarios.md:
   615→   615→	// - New file + content hash mismatch → No overlap → No checkpoint trailer
   616→   616→	assert.Empty(t, checkpointIDs,
   617→   617→		"Content-aware detection should prevent checkpoint trailer when user completely replaces agent content")
   618→   618→}
   619→   619→
   620→   620→// TestE2E_Scenario1_BasicFlow verifies the simplest workflow matches the documented scenario.
   621→   621→//
   622→   622→// Scenario 1: Prompt → Changes → Prompt Finishes → User Commits
   623→   623→// This test explicitly verifies the documented flow.
   624→   624→func TestE2E_Scenario1_BasicFlow(t *testing.T) {
   625→   625→	t.Parallel()
   626→   626→
   627→   627→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   628→   628→
   629→   629→	// 1. User submits prompt (triggers UserPromptSubmit hook → InitializeSession)
   630→   630→	t.Log("Step 1: User submits prompt")
   631→   631→
   632→   632→	// 2. Claude makes changes (creates files)
   633→   633→	prompt := `Create a file called scenario1.go with this content:
   634→   634→package main
   635→   635→func Scenario1() {}
   636→   636→Create only this file.`
   637→   637→
   638→   638→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   639→   639→	require.NoError(t, err)
   640→   640→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   641→   641→
   642→   642→	// Verify file was created
   643→   643→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("scenario1.go"))
   644→   644→
   645→   645→	// 3. After stop hook: checkpoint is saved, FilesTouched is set
   646→   646→	t.Log("Step 3: Checking rewind points after stop")
   647→   647→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   648→   648→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(points), 1, "Should have rewind point after stop")
   649→   649→	t.Logf("Rewind points: %d", len(points))
   650→   650→
   651→   651→	// 4. User commits (triggers prepare-commit-msg and post-commit hooks)
   652→   652→	t.Log("Step 4: User commits")
   653→   653→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add scenario1 file", "scenario1.go")
   654→   654→
   655→   655→	// 5. Verify checkpoint was created with trailer
   656→   656→	checkpointID, err := env.GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory()
   657→   657→	require.NoError(t, err, "Should find checkpoint in commit history")
   658→   658→	assert.NotEmpty(t, checkpointID, "Commit should have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
   659→   659→	t.Logf("Checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointID)
   660→   660→
   661→   661→	// 6. Verify shadow branch was cleaned up and metadata branch exists
   662→   662→	assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   663→   663→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   664→   664→
   665→   665→	// 7. Validate checkpoint has proper metadata and transcript
   666→   666→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   667→   667→		CheckpointID:              checkpointID,
   668→   668→		Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   669→   669→		FilesTouched:              []string{"scenario1.go"},
   670→   670→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"scenario1.go"},
   671→   671→	})
   672→   672→}
   673→   673→
   674→   674→// TestE2E_Scenario2_AgentCommitsDuringTurn verifies the deferred finalization flow.
   675→   675→//
   676→   676→// Scenario 2: Prompt Commits Within Single Turn
   677→   677→// - Agent commits during ACTIVE phase
   678→   678→// - PostCommit saves provisional transcript
   679→   679→// - HandleTurnEnd (Stop hook) finalizes with full transcript
   680→   680→func TestE2E_Scenario2_AgentCommitsDuringTurn(t *testing.T) {
   681→   681→	t.Parallel()
   682→   682→
   683→   683→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   684→   684→
   685→   685→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
   686→   686→
   687→   687→	// Agent creates file and commits it
   688→   688→	t.Log("Agent creating file and committing")
   689→   689→	commitPrompt := `Create a file called agent_commit.go with this content:
   690→   690→package main
   691→   691→func AgentCommit() {}
   692→   692→
   693→   693→Then commit it with: git add agent_commit.go && git commit -m "Agent adds file"
   694→   694→
   695→   695→Create the file first, then run the git commands.`
   696→   696→
   697→   697→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(commitPrompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
   698→   698→	require.NoError(t, err)
   699→   699→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   700→   700→
   701→   701→	// Verify file was created
   702→   702→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_commit.go"))
   703→   703→
   704→   704→	// Verify commit was made
   705→   705→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
   706→   706→	assert.Greater(t, commitsAfter, commitsBefore, "Agent should have made a commit")
   707→   707→
   708→   708→	// Check commit message
   709→   709→	headMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(env.GetHeadHash())
   710→   710→	t.Logf("HEAD commit message: %s", headMsg)
   711→   711→
   712→   712→	// Check for checkpoint trailer
   713→   713→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   714→   714→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   715→   715→
   716→   716→	// Verify metadata branch exists (if checkpoint was created)
   717→   717→	if len(checkpointIDs) > 0 {
   718→   718→		assert.True(t, env.BranchExists("entire/checkpoints/v1"),
   719→   719→			"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist")
   720→   720→
   721→   721→		// Validate checkpoint has proper metadata and transcript
   722→   722→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   723→   723→			CheckpointID:              checkpointIDs[0],
   724→   724→			Strategy:                  "manual-commit",
   725→   725→			FilesTouched:              []string{"agent_commit.go"},
   726→   726→			ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"agent_commit.go"},
   727→   727→		})
   728→   728→	}
   729→   729→}
   730→   730→
   731→   731→// =============================================================================
   732→   732→// Tests for MODIFYING EXISTING FILES (not just creating new ones)
   733→   733→// =============================================================================
   734→   734→// These tests ensure checkpoints work correctly when the agent modifies
   735→   735→// tracked files that already exist in the repository, which behaves differently
   736→   736→// from creating new (untracked) files with respect to git stash, staging, etc.
   737→   737→
   738→   738→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_ModifyAndCommit tests basic workflow where agent
   739→   739→// modifies an existing tracked file instead of creating a new one.
   740→   740→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_ModifyAndCommit(t *testing.T) {
   741→   741→	t.Parallel()
   742→   742→
   743→   743→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   744→   744→
   745→   745→	// Create and commit an existing file first
   746→   746→	env.WriteFile("config.go", `package main
   747→   747→
   748→   748→var Config = map[string]string{
   749→   749→	"version": "1.0",
   750→   750→}
   751→   751→`)
   752→   752→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add initial config", "config.go")
   753→   753→
   754→   754→	// Agent modifies the existing file
   755→   755→	t.Log("Agent modifying existing config.go")
   756→   756→	prompt := `Modify the file config.go to add a new config key "debug" with value "true".
   757→   757→Keep the existing content and just add the new key. Only modify this one file.`
   758→   758→
   759→   759→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   760→   760→	require.NoError(t, err)
   761→   761→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   762→   762→
   763→   763→	// Verify file was modified
   764→   764→	content := env.ReadFile("config.go")
   765→   765→	assert.Contains(t, content, "debug", "config.go should contain debug key")
   766→   766→
   767→   767→	// User commits the modification
   768→   768→	t.Log("User committing modified config.go")
   769→   769→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add debug config", "config.go")
   770→   770→
   771→   771→	// Verify checkpoint was created
   772→   772→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   773→   773→	// First commit (initial config) may or may not have checkpoint depending on session state
   774→   774→	// The agent's modification commit should have a checkpoint
   775→   775→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointIDs, "Should have at least one checkpoint")
   776→   776→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   777→   777→}
   778→   778→
   779→   779→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_StashModifications tests stashing modifications to
   780→   780→// tracked files (not new untracked files). This exercises the standard git stash
   781→   781→// behavior (without -u flag) since modifications to tracked files are stashable.
   782→   782→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_StashModifications(t *testing.T) {
   783→   783→	t.Parallel()
   784→   784→
   785→   785→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   786→   786→
   787→   787→	// Create and commit two existing files
   788→   788→	env.WriteFile("fileA.go", "package main\n\nfunc A() { /* original */ }\n")
   789→   789→	env.WriteFile("fileB.go", "package main\n\nfunc B() { /* original */ }\n")
   790→   790→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add initial files", "fileA.go", "fileB.go")
   791→   791→
   792→   792→	// Agent modifies both files
   793→   793→	t.Log("Agent modifying fileA.go and fileB.go")
   794→   794→	prompt := `Modify these files:
   795→   795→1. In fileA.go, change the comment from "original" to "modified by agent"
   796→   796→2. In fileB.go, change the comment from "original" to "modified by agent"
   797→   797→Only modify these two files.`
   798→   798→
   799→   799→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   800→   800→	require.NoError(t, err)
   801→   801→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   802→   802→
   803→   803→	// Verify both files were modified
   804→   804→	contentA := env.ReadFile("fileA.go")
   805→   805→	contentB := env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   806→   806→	assert.Contains(t, contentA, "modified by agent", "fileA.go should be modified")
   807→   807→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should be modified")
   808→   808→
   809→   809→	// User commits only fileA.go
   810→   810→	t.Log("Committing only fileA.go")
   811→   811→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update fileA", "fileA.go")
   812→   812→	checkpoint1 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   813→   813→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint1, "First commit should have checkpoint")
   814→   814→
   815→   815→	// User stashes fileB.go modifications (tracked file, standard stash works)
   816→   816→	t.Log("Stashing fileB.go modifications")
   817→   817→	env.GitStash()
   818→   818→
   819→   819→	// Verify fileB.go is reverted to original
   820→   820→	contentB = env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   821→   821→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "original", "fileB.go should be reverted after stash")
   822→   822→	assert.NotContains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should not have agent changes")
   823→   823→
   824→   824→	// User pops the stash and commits fileB.go
   825→   825→	t.Log("Popping stash and committing fileB.go")
   826→   826→	env.GitStashPop()
   827→   827→	contentB = env.ReadFile("fileB.go")
   828→   828→	assert.Contains(t, contentB, "modified by agent", "fileB.go should have agent changes after stash pop")
   829→   829→
   830→   830→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update fileB", "fileB.go")
   831→   831→	checkpoint2 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   832→   832→
   833→   833→	// Both commits should have checkpoints
   834→   834→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint2, "Second commit should have checkpoint")
   835→   835→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpoint1, checkpoint2, "Checkpoints should be different")
   836→   836→
   837→   837→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1: %s, Checkpoint 2: %s", checkpoint1, checkpoint2)
   838→   838→}
   839→   839→
   840→   840→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_SplitCommits tests user splitting agent's modifications
   841→   841→// to multiple existing files into separate commits.
   842→   842→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_SplitCommits(t *testing.T) {
   843→   843→	t.Parallel()
   844→   844→
   845→   845→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   846→   846→
   847→   847→	// Create and commit multiple existing files
   848→   848→	env.WriteFile("model.go", "package main\n\ntype Model struct{}\n")
   849→   849→	env.WriteFile("view.go", "package main\n\ntype View struct{}\n")
   850→   850→	env.WriteFile("controller.go", "package main\n\ntype Controller struct{}\n")
   851→   851→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add MVC scaffolding", "model.go", "view.go", "controller.go")
   852→   852→
   853→   853→	// Agent modifies all three files
   854→   854→	t.Log("Agent modifying model.go, view.go, controller.go")
   855→   855→	prompt := `Add a Name field (string type) to each struct in these files:
   856→   856→1. model.go - add Name string to Model struct
   857→   857→2. view.go - add Name string to View struct
   858→   858→3. controller.go - add Name string to Controller struct
   859→   859→Only modify these three files.`
   860→   860→
   861→   861→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   862→   862→	require.NoError(t, err)
   863→   863→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   864→   864→
   865→   865→	// User commits each file separately
   866→   866→	t.Log("Committing model.go")
   867→   867→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to Model", "model.go")
   868→   868→	checkpointModel := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   869→   869→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointModel, "Model commit should have checkpoint")
   870→   870→
   871→   871→	t.Log("Committing view.go")
   872→   872→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to View", "view.go")
   873→   873→	checkpointView := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   874→   874→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointView, "View commit should have checkpoint")
   875→   875→
   876→   876→	t.Log("Committing controller.go")
   877→   877→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add Name to Controller", "controller.go")
   878→   878→	checkpointController := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   879→   879→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpointController, "Controller commit should have checkpoint")
   880→   880→
   881→   881→	// All three checkpoints should be different
   882→   882→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointModel, checkpointView)
   883→   883→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointView, checkpointController)
   884→   884→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointModel, checkpointController)
   885→   885→
   886→   886→	t.Logf("Checkpoints: model=%s, view=%s, controller=%s",
   887→   887→		checkpointModel, checkpointView, checkpointController)
   888→   888→
   889→   889→	// Validate each checkpoint has the correct files_touched
   890→   890→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   891→   891→		CheckpointID: checkpointModel,
   892→   892→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   893→   893→		FilesTouched: []string{"model.go"},
   894→   894→	})
   895→   895→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   896→   896→		CheckpointID: checkpointView,
   897→   897→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   898→   898→		FilesTouched: []string{"view.go"},
   899→   899→	})
   900→   900→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   901→   901→		CheckpointID: checkpointController,
   902→   902→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
   903→   903→		FilesTouched: []string{"controller.go"},
   904→   904→	})
   905→   905→
   906→   906→	// No shadow branches should remain after all files committed
   907→   907→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
   908→   908→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
   909→   909→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
   910→   910→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
   911→   911→	}
   912→   912→}
   913→   913→
   914→   914→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_RevertModification tests that modifying an existing file
   915→   915→// that was touched by the session ALWAYS gets a checkpoint, even if the user writes
   916→   916→// completely different content.
   917→   917→//
   918→   918→// This is intentional behavior: for files that already exist in HEAD (modified files),
   919→   919→// we always count as overlap because the user is editing a file the session worked on.
   920→   920→// Content-aware detection only applies to NEW files (don't exist in HEAD).
   921→   921→//
   922→   922→// See content_overlap.go for the rationale.
   923→   923→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_RevertModification(t *testing.T) {
   924→   924→	t.Parallel()
   925→   925→
   926→   926→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   927→   927→
   928→   928→	// Create and commit an existing file
   929→   929→	originalContent := `package main
   930→   930→
   931→   931→// placeholder
   932→   932→`
   933→   933→	env.WriteFile("calc.go", originalContent)
   934→   934→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add placeholder", "calc.go")
   935→   935→
   936→   936→	// Agent modifies the existing file
   937→   937→	t.Log("Agent modifying calc.go")
   938→   938→	prompt := `Replace the contents of calc.go with this exact code:
   939→   939→package main
   940→   940→
   941→   941→func AgentMultiply(a, b int) int {
   942→   942→	return a * b
   943→   943→}
   944→   944→
   945→   945→Only modify calc.go, nothing else.`
   946→   946→
   947→   947→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
   948→   948→	require.NoError(t, err)
   949→   949→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   950→   950→
   951→   951→	// Verify agent modified the file
   952→   952→	modifiedContent := env.ReadFile("calc.go")
   953→   953→	assert.Contains(t, modifiedContent, "AgentMultiply", "calc.go should have AgentMultiply function")
   954→   954→
   955→   955→	// User reverts and writes completely different content
   956→   956→	t.Log("User reverting and writing completely different content")
   957→   957→	userContent := `package main
   958→   958→
   959→   959→func UserAdd(x, y int) int {
   960→   960→	return x + y
   961→   961→}
   962→   962→`
   963→   963→	env.WriteFile("calc.go", userContent)
   964→   964→
   965→   965→	// User commits their changes
   966→   966→	t.Log("Committing user's changes")
   967→   967→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user functions", "calc.go")
   968→   968→
   969→   969→	// For MODIFIED files (existing in HEAD), we always count as overlap.
   970→   970→	// The checkpoint SHOULD be created even though content is different.
   971→   971→	// This is because the session touched this file, so any commit to it
   972→   972→	// should be linked to preserve the editing history.
   973→   973→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   974→   974→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDs)
   975→   975→
   976→   976→	latestCheckpoint := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
   977→   977→	assert.NotEmpty(t, latestCheckpoint,
   978→   978→		"Modified files should always get checkpoint (content-aware only applies to new files)")
   979→   979→}
   980→   980→
   981→   981→// TestE2E_ExistingFiles_MixedNewAndModified tests a scenario where the agent
   982→   982→// both creates new files AND modifies existing files in the same session.
   983→   983→func TestE2E_ExistingFiles_MixedNewAndModified(t *testing.T) {
   984→   984→	t.Parallel()
   985→   985→
   986→   986→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   987→   987→
   988→   988→	// Create and commit an existing file
   989→   989→	env.WriteFile("main.go", `package main
   990→   990→
   991→   991→func main() {
   992→   992→	// TODO: add imports
   993→   993→}
   994→   994→`)
   995→   995→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add main.go", "main.go")
   996→   996→
   997→   997→	// Agent modifies existing file AND creates new files
   998→   998→	t.Log("Agent modifying main.go and creating new files")
   999→   999→	prompt := `Do these tasks:
  1000→  1000→1. Create a new file utils.go with: package main; func Helper() string { return "helper" }
  1001→  1001→2. Create a new file types.go with: package main; type Config struct { Name string }
  1002→  1002→3. Modify main.go to add a comment "// imports utils and types" at the top (after package main)
  1003→  1003→Complete all three tasks.`
  1004→  1004→
  1005→  1005→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1006→  1006→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1007→  1007→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1008→  1008→
  1009→  1009→	// Verify all changes
  1010→  1010→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("utils.go"), "utils.go should exist")
  1011→  1011→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("types.go"), "types.go should exist")
  1012→  1012→	mainContent := env.ReadFile("main.go")
  1013→  1013→	assert.Contains(t, mainContent, "imports utils and types", "main.go should be modified")
  1014→  1014→
  1015→  1015→	// User commits existing file modification first
  1016→  1016→	t.Log("Committing main.go modification")
  1017→  1017→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Update main.go imports comment", "main.go")
  1018→  1018→	checkpoint1 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
  1019→  1019→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint1, "main.go commit should have checkpoint")
  1020→  1020→
  1021→  1021→	// User commits new files together
  1022→  1022→	t.Log("Committing new files utils.go and types.go")
  1023→  1023→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add utils and types", "utils.go", "types.go")
  1024→  1024→	checkpoint2 := env.GetLatestCheckpointID()
  1025→  1025→	require.NotEmpty(t, checkpoint2, "New files commit should have checkpoint")
  1026→  1026→
  1027→  1027→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpoint1, checkpoint2, "Checkpoints should be different")
  1028→  1028→	t.Logf("Checkpoint 1 (modified): %s, Checkpoint 2 (new): %s", checkpoint1, checkpoint2)
  1029→  1029→}
  1030→  1030→
  1031→  1031→// TestE2E_EndedSession_UserCommitsAfterExit tests that after agent exits
  1032→  1032→// (session naturally ends), user commits still get checkpoint trailers.
  1033→  1033→// This exercises the ENDED + GitCommit → ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched path.
  1034→  1034→func TestE2E_EndedSession_UserCommitsAfterExit(t *testing.T) {
  1035→  1035→	t.Parallel()
  1036→  1036→
  1037→  1037→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1038→  1038→
  1039→  1039→	// Agent creates files A, B, C — session ends when agent exits
  1040→  1040→	prompt := `Create these files:
  1041→  1041→1. ended_a.go with content: package main; func EndedA() {}
  1042→  1042→2. ended_b.go with content: package main; func EndedB() {}
  1043→  1043→3. ended_c.go with content: package main; func EndedC() {}
  1044→  1044→Create all three files, nothing else.`
  1045→  1045→
  1046→  1046→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1047→  1047→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1048→  1048→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1049→  1049→
  1050→  1050→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("ended_a.go"), "ended_a.go should exist")
  1051→  1051→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("ended_b.go"), "ended_b.go should exist")
  1052→  1052→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("ended_c.go"), "ended_c.go should exist")
  1053→  1053→
  1054→  1054→	// User commits A and B together
  1055→  1055→	t.Log("Committing ended_a.go and ended_b.go")
  1056→  1056→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add ended files A and B", "ended_a.go", "ended_b.go")
  1057→  1057→	checkpointIDsAfterFirst := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1058→  1058→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterFirst), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
  1059→  1059→	checkpointAB := checkpointIDsAfterFirst[0]
  1060→  1060→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for A,B: %s", checkpointAB)
  1061→  1061→
  1062→  1062→	// User commits C
  1063→  1063→	t.Log("Committing ended_c.go")
  1064→  1064→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add ended file C", "ended_c.go")
  1065→  1065→	checkpointIDsAfterSecond := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1066→  1066→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterSecond), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
  1067→  1067→	checkpointC := checkpointIDsAfterSecond[0]
  1068→  1068→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for C: %s", checkpointC)
  1069→  1069→
  1070→  1070→	// Checkpoint IDs must be unique
  1071→  1071→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointAB, checkpointC,
  1072→  1072→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
  1073→  1073→
  1074→  1074→	// Validate both checkpoints
  1075→  1075→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1076→  1076→		CheckpointID: checkpointAB,
  1077→  1077→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1078→  1078→		FilesTouched: []string{"ended_a.go", "ended_b.go"},
  1079→  1079→	})
  1080→  1080→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1081→  1081→		CheckpointID: checkpointC,
  1082→  1082→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1083→  1083→		FilesTouched: []string{"ended_c.go"},
  1084→  1084→	})
  1085→  1085→
  1086→  1086→	// No shadow branches should remain
  1087→  1087→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
  1088→  1088→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
  1089→  1089→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
  1090→  1090→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
  1091→  1091→	}
  1092→  1092→}
  1093→  1093→
  1094→  1094→// TestE2E_DeletedFiles_CommitDeletion tests that deleting a file that was tracked
  1095→  1095→// in the session gets handled properly when committed via git rm.
  1096→  1096→func TestE2E_DeletedFiles_CommitDeletion(t *testing.T) {
  1097→  1097→	t.Parallel()
  1098→  1098→
  1099→  1099→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1100→  1100→
  1101→  1101→	// Pre-commit a file that will be deleted
  1102→  1102→	env.WriteFile("to_delete.go", "package main\n\nfunc ToDelete() {}\n")
  1103→  1103→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add to_delete.go", "to_delete.go")
  1104→  1104→
  1105→  1105→	// Agent deletes it and creates a replacement
  1106→  1106→	prompt := `Do these two tasks:
  1107→  1107→1. Delete the file to_delete.go (use: rm to_delete.go)
  1108→  1108→2. Create a new file replacement.go with content: package main; func Replacement() {}
  1109→  1109→
  1110→  1110→Do both tasks.`
  1111→  1111→
  1112→  1112→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
  1113→  1113→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1114→  1114→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1115→  1115→
  1116→  1116→	// to_delete.go should be gone, replacement.go should exist
  1117→  1117→	assert.False(t, env.FileExists("to_delete.go"), "to_delete.go should be deleted")
  1118→  1118→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("replacement.go"), "replacement.go should exist")
  1119→  1119→
  1120→  1120→	// Commit the replacement file first
  1121→  1121→	t.Log("Committing replacement.go")
  1122→  1122→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add replacement", "replacement.go")
  1123→  1123→	checkpointIDsAfterFirst := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1124→  1124→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfterFirst), 1, "Should have checkpoint for replacement")
  1125→  1125→	checkpointReplacement := checkpointIDsAfterFirst[0]
  1126→  1126→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for replacement: %s", checkpointReplacement)
  1127→  1127→
  1128→  1128→	// Commit the deletion via git rm
  1129→  1129→	t.Log("Committing deletion of to_delete.go")
  1130→  1130→	env.GitRm("to_delete.go")
  1131→  1131→	env.GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks("Remove to_delete.go")
  1132→  1132→
  1133→  1133→	// Check if deletion got a checkpoint trailer
  1134→  1134→	checkpointIDsAfterSecond := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1135→  1135→	if len(checkpointIDsAfterSecond) > len(checkpointIDsAfterFirst) {
  1136→  1136→		checkpointDeletion := checkpointIDsAfterSecond[0]
  1137→  1137→		t.Logf("Checkpoint for deletion: %s (carry-forward for deleted file)", checkpointDeletion)
  1138→  1138→		assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointReplacement, checkpointDeletion,
  1139→  1139→			"Checkpoint IDs should be unique")
  1140→  1140→		env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1141→  1141→			CheckpointID: checkpointDeletion,
  1142→  1142→			Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1143→  1143→		})
  1144→  1144→	} else {
  1145→  1145→		t.Log("Deletion commit has no checkpoint trailer (deleted files may not carry forward)")
  1146→  1146→	}
  1147→  1147→
  1148→  1148→	// Validate the replacement checkpoint
  1149→  1149→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1150→  1150→		CheckpointID: checkpointReplacement,
  1151→  1151→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1152→  1152→	})
  1153→  1153→}
  1154→  1154→
  1155→  1155→// TestE2E_AgentCommitsMidTurn_UserCommitsRemainder tests that when the agent
  1156→  1156→// commits some files during its turn and the user commits the rest after,
  1157→  1157→// both get valid checkpoint trailers.
  1158→  1158→func TestE2E_AgentCommitsMidTurn_UserCommitsRemainder(t *testing.T) {
  1159→  1159→	t.Parallel()
  1160→  1160→
  1161→  1161→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1162→  1162→
  1163→  1163→	commitsBefore := env.GetCommitCount()
  1164→  1164→
  1165→  1165→	// Agent creates 3 files and commits 2 mid-turn
  1166→  1166→	prompt := `Do these tasks in order:
  1167→  1167→1. Create file agent_mid1.go with content: package main; func AgentMid1() {}
  1168→  1168→2. Create file agent_mid2.go with content: package main; func AgentMid2() {}
  1169→  1169→3. Commit these two files: git add agent_mid1.go agent_mid2.go && git commit -m "Agent adds mid1 and mid2"
  1170→  1170→4. Create file user_remainder.go with content: package main; func UserRemainder() {}
  1171→  1171→
  1172→  1172→Do all tasks in order. Create each file, then commit the first two, then create the third.`
  1173→  1173→
  1174→  1174→	result, err := env.RunAgentWithTools(prompt, []string{"Write", "Bash"})
  1175→  1175→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1176→  1176→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1177→  1177→
  1178→  1178→	// Verify all files were created
  1179→  1179→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_mid1.go"), "agent_mid1.go should exist")
  1180→  1180→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("agent_mid2.go"), "agent_mid2.go should exist")
  1181→  1181→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("user_remainder.go"), "user_remainder.go should exist")
  1182→  1182→
  1183→  1183→	// Verify agent made a commit
  1184→  1184→	commitsAfter := env.GetCommitCount()
  1185→  1185→	assert.Greater(t, commitsAfter, commitsBefore, "Agent should have made a commit")
  1186→  1186→
  1187→  1187→	// Get agent checkpoint IDs
  1188→  1188→	agentCheckpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1189→  1189→	t.Logf("Agent checkpoint IDs: %v", agentCheckpointIDs)
  1190→  1190→
  1191→  1191→	// User commits the remaining file
  1192→  1192→	t.Log("User committing user_remainder.go")
  1193→  1193→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add user remainder", "user_remainder.go")
  1194→  1194→
  1195→  1195→	// Get all checkpoint IDs including user's
  1196→  1196→	allCheckpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1197→  1197→	t.Logf("All checkpoint IDs: %v", allCheckpointIDs)
  1198→  1198→
  1199→  1199→	// User's checkpoint should exist and be different from agent's
  1200→  1200→	require.Greater(t, len(allCheckpointIDs), len(agentCheckpointIDs),
  1201→  1201→		"User commit should have added a new checkpoint")
  1202→  1202→
  1203→  1203→	userCheckpointID := allCheckpointIDs[0] // Most recent
  1204→  1204→	t.Logf("User checkpoint ID: %s", userCheckpointID)
  1205→  1205→
  1206→  1206→	// User checkpoint must be different from all agent checkpoints
  1207→  1207→	for _, agentID := range agentCheckpointIDs {
  1208→  1208→		assert.NotEqual(t, userCheckpointID, agentID,
  1209→  1209→			"User checkpoint should be different from agent checkpoint")
  1210→  1210→	}
  1211→  1211→
  1212→  1212→	// Validate user's checkpoint exists on metadata branch
  1213→  1213→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
  1214→  1214→		CheckpointID: userCheckpointID,
  1215→  1215→		Strategy:     "manual-commit",
  1216→  1216→		FilesTouched: []string{"user_remainder.go"},
  1217→  1217→	})
  1218→  1218→
  1219→  1219→	// No shadow branches should remain
  1220→  1220→	entireBranches := env.ListBranchesWithPrefix("entire/")
  1221→  1221→	for _, b := range entireBranches {
  1222→  1222→		assert.Equal(t, "entire/checkpoints/v1", b,
  1223→  1223→			"Unexpected shadow branch after all files committed: %s", b)
  1224→  1224→	}
  1225→  1225→}
  1226→  1226→
  1227→  1227→// TestE2E_TrailerRemoval_SkipsCondensation tests that when a user removes the
  1228→  1228→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer from the commit message, no condensation happens.
  1229→  1229→func TestE2E_TrailerRemoval_SkipsCondensation(t *testing.T) {
  1230→  1230→	t.Parallel()
  1231→  1231→
  1232→  1232→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1233→  1233→
  1234→  1234→	// Agent creates a file
  1235→  1235→	prompt := `Create a file called trailer_test.go with content:
  1236→  1236→package main
  1237→  1237→func TrailerTest() {}
  1238→  1238→Create only this file.`
  1239→  1239→
  1240→  1240→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1241→  1241→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1242→  1242→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1243→  1243→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("trailer_test.go"), "trailer_test.go should exist")
  1244→  1244→
  1245→  1245→	// Count checkpoints before
  1246→  1246→	checkpointsBefore := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1247→  1247→
  1248→  1248→	// User commits with trailer removed (simulating user editing out the trailer)
  1249→  1249→	t.Log("Committing with trailer removed")
  1250→  1250→	env.GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved("Add trailer_test (no checkpoint)", "trailer_test.go")
  1251→  1251→
  1252→  1252→	// Verify the commit message does NOT have the trailer
  1253→  1253→	headMsg := env.GetCommitMessage(env.GetHeadHash())
  1254→  1254→	assert.NotContains(t, headMsg, "Entire-Checkpoint:",
  1255→  1255→		"Commit message should not have Entire-Checkpoint trailer")
  1256→  1256→
  1257→  1257→	// No new checkpoint should have been created
  1258→  1258→	checkpointsAfter := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1259→  1259→	assert.Len(t, checkpointsAfter, len(checkpointsBefore),
  1260→  1260→		"No new checkpoint should have been created when trailer is removed")
  1261→  1261→}
  1262→  1262→
  1263→  1263→// TestE2E_SessionDepleted_ManualEditNoCheckpoint tests that once all session
  1264→  1264→// files are committed, subsequent manual edits do NOT get checkpoint trailers.
  1265→  1265→func TestE2E_SessionDepleted_ManualEditNoCheckpoint(t *testing.T) {
  1266→  1266→	t.Parallel()
  1267→  1267→
  1268→  1268→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
  1269→  1269→
  1270→  1270→	// Agent creates a file
  1271→  1271→	prompt := `Create a file called depleted.go with content:
  1272→  1272→package main
  1273→  1273→func Depleted() {}
  1274→  1274→Create only this file.`
  1275→  1275→
  1276→  1276→	result, err := env.RunAgent(prompt)
  1277→  1277→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1278→  1278→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
  1279→  1279→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("depleted.go"), "depleted.go should exist")
  1280→  1280→
  1281→  1281→	// User commits the agent's file → gets checkpoint
  1282→  1282→	t.Log("Committing agent's file")
  1283→  1283→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add depleted.go", "depleted.go")
  1284→  1284→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1285→  1285→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 1, "Should have checkpoint for agent's file")
  1286→  1286→	t.Logf("Checkpoint for agent's file: %s", checkpointIDs[0])
  1287→  1287→
  1288→  1288→	// User manually edits the file (not from agent)
  1289→  1289→	env.WriteFile("depleted.go", "package main\n\n// Manual user edit\nfunc Depleted() { return }\n")
  1290→  1290→
  1291→  1291→	// User commits the manual edit
  1292→  1292→	t.Log("Committing manual edit")
  1293→  1293→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Manual edit to depleted.go", "depleted.go")
  1294→  1294→
  1295→  1295→	// This commit should NOT have a new checkpoint — the session is depleted
  1296→  1296→	// (all agent files were already committed) and the new edit is purely manual.
  1297→  1297→	allCheckpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
  1298→  1298→	assert.Len(t, allCheckpointIDs, len(checkpointIDs),
  1299→  1299→		"Manual edit after session depletion should not create a new checkpoint")
  1300→  1300→}

    24→    24→// defaultAgent holds the agent to test with, determined in TestMain.
    25→    25→var defaultAgent string
    26→    26→
    27→    27→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    28→    28→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.
    29→    29→var testBinaryPath string
    30→    30→
    31→    31→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for E2E tests with real agent calls.
    32→    32→type TestEnv struct {
    33→    33→	T       *testing.T
    34→    34→	RepoDir string
    35→    35→	Agent   AgentRunner
    36→    36→}
    37→    37→
    38→    38→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    39→    39→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    40→    40→func getTestBinary() string {
    41→    41→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    42→    42→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    43→    43→	}
    44→    44→	return testBinaryPath
    45→    45→}
    46→    46→
    47→    47→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated E2E test environment.
    48→    48→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    49→    49→	t.Helper()
    50→    50→
    51→    51→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    52→    52→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    53→    53→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    54→    54→		repoDir = resolved
    55→    55→	}
    56→    56→
    57→    57→	// Create agent runner
    58→    58→	agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    59→    59→
    60→    60→	return &TestEnv{
    61→    61→		T:       t,
    62→    62→		RepoDir: repoDir,
    63→    63→		Agent:   agent,
    64→    64→	}
    65→    65→}
    66→    66→
    67→    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→    71→	t.Helper()
    72→    72→
    73→    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→    79→
    80→    80→	// OpenCode's `run` (non-interactive) mode defaults external_directory permission
    81→    81→	// to "ask", which auto-rejects since there's no user to prompt — even when the
    82→    82→	// path is the project directory itself. Override to "allow" for the test repo.
    83→    83→	// Note: specific path patterns don't work here because OpenCode evaluates the
    84→    84→	// catch-all "ask" rule before specific "allow" rules (known issue).
    85→    85→	// Include $schema to prevent OpenCode from modifying the file when it runs.
    86→    86→	if defaultAgent == AgentNameOpenCode {
    87→    87→		env.WriteFile("opencode.json", `{
    88→    88→  "$schema": "https://opencode.ai/config.json",
    89→    89→  "permission": {
    90→    90→    "external_directory": "allow"
    91→    91→  }
    92→    92→}
    93→    93→`)
    94→    94→	}
    95→    95→
    96→    96→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    97→    97→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    98→    98→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    99→    99→
   100→   100→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
   101→   101→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
   102→   102→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
   103→   103→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
   104→   104→	env.GitAddAll()
   105→   105→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
   106→   106→
   107→   107→	return env
   108→   108→}
   109→   109→
   110→   110→// RunEntireEnable runs `entire enable` to set up the project with hooks.
   111→   111→// Uses the configured defaultAgent (from E2E_AGENT env var or "claude-code").
   112→   112→func (env *TestEnv) RunEntireEnable(strategyName string) {
   113→   113→	env.T.Helper()
   114→   114→
   115→   115→	args := []string{
   116→   116→		"enable",
   117→   117→		"--agent", defaultAgent,
   118→   118→		"--strategy", strategyName,
   119→   119→		"--telemetry=false",
   120→   120→		"--force", // Force reinstall hooks in case they exist
   121→   121→	}
   122→   122→
   123→   123→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static
   124→   124→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), args...)
   125→   125→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   126→   126→
   127→   127→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   128→   128→	if err != nil {
   129→   129→		env.T.Fatalf("entire enable failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   130→   130→	}
   131→   131→	env.T.Logf("entire enable output: %s", output)
   132→   132→}

   134→   134→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.
   135→   135→func (env *TestEnv) InitRepo() {
   136→   136→	env.T.Helper()
   137→   137→
   138→   138→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   139→   139→	if err != nil {
   140→   140→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   141→   141→	}
   142→   142→
   143→   143→	// Configure git user for commits
   144→   144→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   145→   145→	if err != nil {
   146→   146→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   147→   147→	}
   148→   148→	cfg.User.Name = "E2E Test User"
   149→   149→	cfg.User.Email = "e2e-test@example.com"
   150→   150→
   151→   151→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   152→   152→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   153→   153→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   154→   154→	}
   155→   155→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   156→   156→
   157→   157→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   158→   158→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   159→   159→	}
   160→   160→}

   162→   162→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.
   163→   163→func (env *TestEnv) WriteFile(path, content string) {
   164→   164→	env.T.Helper()
   165→   165→
   166→   166→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   167→   167→
   168→   168→	// Create parent directories
   169→   169→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   170→   170→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   171→   171→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   172→   172→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   173→   173→	}
   174→   174→
   175→   175→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   176→   176→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   177→   177→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   178→   178→	}
   179→   179→}

   216→   216→// GitAdd stages files for commit.

   235→   235→}

   274→   274→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, running the prepare-commit-msg
   275→   275→// and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   276→   276→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   277→   277→	env.T.Helper()
   278→   278→
   279→   279→	// Stage files using go-git
   280→   280→	for _, file := range files {
   281→   281→		env.GitAdd(file)
   282→   282→	}
   283→   283→
   284→   284→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   285→   285→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   286→   286→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   287→   287→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   288→   288→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   289→   289→	}
   290→   290→
   291→   291→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   292→   292→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   293→   293→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   294→   294→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   295→   295→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   296→   296→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   297→   297→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   298→   298→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   299→   299→	)
   300→   300→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   301→   301→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   302→   302→	}
   303→   303→
   304→   304→	// Read the modified message
   305→   305→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   306→   306→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   307→   307→	if err != nil {
   308→   308→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   309→   309→	}
   310→   310→
   311→   311→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   312→   312→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   313→   313→	if err != nil {
   314→   314→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   315→   315→	}
   316→   316→
   317→   317→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   318→   318→	if err != nil {
   319→   319→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   320→   320→	}
   321→   321→
   322→   322→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   323→   323→		Author: &object.Signature{
   324→   324→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   325→   325→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   326→   326→			When:  time.Now(),
   327→   327→		},
   328→   328→	})
   329→   329→	if err != nil {
   330→   330→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   331→   331→	}
   332→   332→
   333→   333→	// Run post-commit hook
   334→   334→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   335→   335→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   336→   336→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   337→   337→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   338→   338→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   339→   339→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   340→   340→	)
   341→   341→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   342→   342→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   343→   343→	}
   344→   344→}

   375→   375→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir

   384→   384→
   385→   385→	// Read the modified message

   388→   388→	if err != nil {

   391→   391→
   392→   392→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message

   394→   394→	if err != nil {
