   401→   401→	}
   402→   402→
   403→   403→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   404→   404→		Author: &object.Signature{
   405→   405→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   406→   406→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   407→   407→			When:  time.Now(),
   408→   408→		},
   409→   409→	})
   410→   410→	if err != nil {
   411→   411→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   412→   412→	}
   413→   413→
   414→   414→	// Run post-commit hook
   415→   415→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   416→   416→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   417→   417→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   418→   418→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   419→   419→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   420→   420→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   421→   421→	)
   422→   422→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {

   426→   426→
   427→   427→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
   428→   428→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
   429→   429→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
   430→   430→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
   431→   431→	env.T.Helper()
   432→   432→
   433→   433→	// Stage files using go-git
   434→   434→	for _, file := range files {
   435→   435→		env.GitAdd(file)
   436→   436→	}
   437→   437→
   438→   438→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   439→   439→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   440→   440→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   441→   441→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   442→   442→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   443→   443→	}
   444→   444→
   445→   445→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   446→   446→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   447→   447→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   448→   448→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   449→   449→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   450→   450→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   451→   451→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   452→   452→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   453→   453→	)
   454→   454→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   455→   455→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   456→   456→	}
   457→   457→
   458→   458→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
   459→   459→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   460→   460→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   461→   461→	if err != nil {
   462→   462→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   463→   463→	}
   464→   464→
   465→   465→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
   466→   466→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
   467→   467→	var cleanedLines []string
   468→   468→	for _, line := range lines {
   469→   469→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   470→   470→			continue
   471→   471→		}
   472→   472→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
   473→   473→			continue
   474→   474→		}
   475→   475→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
   476→   476→			continue
   477→   477→		}
   478→   478→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
   479→   479→	}
   480→   480→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
   481→   481→
   482→   482→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
   483→   483→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   484→   484→	if err != nil {
   485→   485→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   486→   486→	}
   487→   487→
   488→   488→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   489→   489→	if err != nil {
   490→   490→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   491→   491→	}
   492→   492→
   493→   493→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   494→   494→		Author: &object.Signature{
   495→   495→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   496→   496→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   497→   497→			When:  time.Now(),
   498→   498→		},
   499→   499→	})
   500→   500→	if err != nil {
   501→   501→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   502→   502→	}
   503→   503→
   504→   504→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
   505→   505→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   506→   506→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   507→   507→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   508→   508→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   509→   509→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   510→   510→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   511→   511→	)
   512→   512→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   513→   513→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   514→   514→	}
   515→   515→}
   516→   516→
   517→   517→// GitCheckoutNewBranch creates and checks out a new branch.
   518→   518→func (env *TestEnv) GitCheckoutNewBranch(branchName string) {
   519→   519→	env.T.Helper()
   520→   520→
   521→   521→	//nolint:noctx // test code, no context needed for git checkout
   522→   522→	cmd := exec.Command("git", "checkout", "-b", branchName)
   523→   523→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   524→   524→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   525→   525→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to checkout new branch %s: %v\nOutput: %s", branchName, err, output)
   526→   526→	}
   527→   527→}

   546→   546→// RewindPoint mirrors the rewind --list JSON output.
   547→   547→type RewindPoint struct {
   548→   548→	ID               string    `json:"id"`
   549→   549→	Message          string    `json:"message"`
   550→   550→	MetadataDir      string    `json:"metadata_dir"`
   551→   551→	Date             time.Time `json:"date"`
   552→   552→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool      `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   553→   553→	ToolUseID        string    `json:"tool_use_id"`
   554→   554→	IsLogsOnly       bool      `json:"is_logs_only"`
   555→   555→	CondensationID   string    `json:"condensation_id"`
   556→   556→}
   557→   557→
   558→   558→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   559→   559→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   560→   560→	env.T.Helper()
   561→   561→
   562→   562→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static, no context needed
   563→   563→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   564→   564→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   565→   565→
   566→   566→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   567→   567→	if err != nil {
   568→   568→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   569→   569→	}
   570→   570→
   571→   571→	// Parse JSON output
   572→   572→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   573→   573→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   574→   574→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   575→   575→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   576→   576→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   577→   577→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   578→   578→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   579→   579→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   580→   580→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   581→   581→	}
   582→   582→
   583→   583→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   584→   584→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   585→   585→	}
   586→   586→
   587→   587→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   588→   588→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   589→   589→		//nolint:errcheck // date parsing failure is acceptable, defaults to zero time
   590→   590→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   591→   591→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   592→   592→			ID:               jp.ID,
   593→   593→			Message:          jp.Message,
   594→   594→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   595→   595→			Date:             date,
   596→   596→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   597→   597→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   598→   598→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   599→   599→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   600→   600→		}
   601→   601→	}
   602→   602→
   603→   603→	return points
   604→   604→}
   605→   605→
   606→   606→// Rewind performs a rewind to the specified commit ID using the CLI.

   611→   611→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--to", commitID)
   612→   612→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir

   618→   618→

   623→   623→// BranchExists checks if a branch exists in the repository.
   624→   624→func (env *TestEnv) BranchExists(branchName string) bool {
   625→   625→	env.T.Helper()
   626→   626→
   627→   627→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   628→   628→	if err != nil {
   629→   629→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   630→   630→	}
   631→   631→
   632→   632→	refs, err := repo.References()
   633→   633→	if err != nil {
   634→   634→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   635→   635→	}
   636→   636→
   637→   637→	found := false
   638→   638→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   639→   639→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   640→   640→		if ref.Name().Short() == branchName {
   641→   641→			found = true
   642→   642→		}
   643→   643→		return nil
   644→   644→	})
   645→   645→
   646→   646→	return found
   647→   647→}
   648→   648→
   649→   649→// ListBranchesWithPrefix returns all branches that start with the given prefix.
   650→   650→func (env *TestEnv) ListBranchesWithPrefix(prefix string) []string {
   651→   651→	env.T.Helper()
   652→   652→
   653→   653→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   654→   654→	if err != nil {
   655→   655→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   656→   656→	}
   657→   657→
   658→   658→	refs, err := repo.References()
   659→   659→	if err != nil {
   660→   660→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get references: %v", err)
   661→   661→	}
   662→   662→
   663→   663→	var branches []string
   664→   664→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   665→   665→	refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error {
   666→   666→		name := ref.Name().Short()
   667→   667→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   668→   668→			branches = append(branches, name)
   669→   669→		}
   670→   670→		return nil
   671→   671→	})
   672→   672→
   673→   673→	return branches
   674→   674→}

   676→   676→// GetCommitMessage returns the commit message for the given commit hash.
   677→   677→func (env *TestEnv) GetCommitMessage(hash string) string {
   678→   678→	env.T.Helper()
   679→   679→
   680→   680→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   681→   681→	if err != nil {
   682→   682→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   683→   683→	}
   684→   684→
   685→   685→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(hash)
   686→   686→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   687→   687→	if err != nil {

   692→   692→}
   693→   693→
   694→   694→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   695→   695→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   696→   696→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   697→   697→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   698→   698→	env.T.Helper()
   699→   699→
   700→   700→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   701→   701→	if err != nil {
   702→   702→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   703→   703→	}
   704→   704→
   705→   705→	head, err := repo.Head()
   706→   706→	if err != nil {
   707→   707→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   708→   708→	}
   709→   709→
   710→   710→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   711→   711→	if err != nil {
   712→   712→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   713→   713→	}
   714→   714→
   715→   715→	var checkpointID string
   716→   716→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   717→   717→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   718→   718→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   719→   719→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   720→   720→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   721→   721→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   722→   722→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   723→   723→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   724→   724→			}
   725→   725→		}
   726→   726→		return nil
   727→   727→	})
   728→   728→
   729→   729→	if checkpointID == "" {
   730→   730→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   731→   731→	}
   732→   732→
   733→   733→	return checkpointID, nil
   734→   734→}
   735→   735→
   736→   736→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   737→   737→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   738→   738→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   739→   739→	env.T.Helper()
   740→   740→
   741→   741→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   742→   742→	if err != nil {
   743→   743→		return ""
   744→   744→	}
   745→   745→
   746→   746→	head, err := repo.Head()
   747→   747→	if err != nil {
   748→   748→		return ""
   749→   749→	}
   750→   750→
   751→   751→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   752→   752→	if err != nil {
   753→   753→		return ""
   754→   754→	}
   755→   755→
   756→   756→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   757→   757→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   758→   758→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   759→   759→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   760→   760→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   761→   761→		}
   762→   762→	}
   763→   763→	return ""
   764→   764→}
   765→   765→
   766→   766→// safeIDPrefix returns first 12 chars of ID or the full ID if shorter.
   767→   767→// Use this when logging checkpoint IDs to avoid index out of bounds panic.
   768→   768→func safeIDPrefix(id string) string {
   769→   769→	if len(id) >= 12 {
   770→   770→		return id[:12]
   771→   771→	}
   772→   772→	return id
   773→   773→}

   797→   797→// RunAgent runs the agent with the given prompt and returns the result.
   798→   798→func (env *TestEnv) RunAgent(prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   799→   799→	env.T.Helper()
   800→   800→	result, err := env.Agent.RunPrompt(context.Background(), env.RepoDir, prompt)
   801→   801→	if err != nil && result != nil {
   802→   802→		env.T.Logf("Agent failed with exit code %d", result.ExitCode)
   803→   803→		if result.Stderr != "" {
   804→   804→			env.T.Logf("Agent stderr: %s", result.Stderr)
   805→   805→		}
   806→   806→		if result.Stdout != "" {
   807→   807→			env.T.Logf("Agent stdout: %s", result.Stdout)
   808→   808→		}
   809→   809→	}
   810→   810→	//nolint:wrapcheck // test helper, caller handles error
   811→   811→	return result, err
   812→   812→}
   813→   813→
   814→   814→// RunAgentWithTools runs the agent with specific tools enabled.
   815→   815→func (env *TestEnv) RunAgentWithTools(prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   816→   816→	env.T.Helper()
   817→   817→	result, err := env.Agent.RunPromptWithTools(context.Background(), env.RepoDir, prompt, tools)
   818→   818→	if err != nil && result != nil {
   819→   819→		env.T.Logf("Agent failed with exit code %d", result.ExitCode)
   820→   820→		if result.Stderr != "" {
   821→   821→			env.T.Logf("Agent stderr: %s", result.Stderr)
   822→   822→		}
   823→   823→		if result.Stdout != "" {
   824→   824→			env.T.Logf("Agent stdout: %s", result.Stdout)
   825→   825→		}
   826→   826→	}
   827→   827→	//nolint:wrapcheck // test helper, caller handles error
   828→   828→	return result, err
   829→   829→}

   907→   907→// GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns

   909→   909→func (env *TestEnv) GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory() []string {
   910→   910→	env.T.Helper()

   912→   912→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   913→   913→	if err != nil {
   914→   914→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   915→   915→	}
   916→   916→
   917→   917→	head, err := repo.Head()
   918→   918→	if err != nil {
   919→   919→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   920→   920→	}
   921→   921→
   922→   922→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   923→   923→	if err != nil {
   924→   924→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   925→   925→	}
   926→   926→
   927→   927→	var checkpointIDs []string
   928→   928→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback doesn't return errors we need to handle
   929→   929→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   930→   930→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   931→   931→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   932→   932→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   933→   933→				id := strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   934→   934→				checkpointIDs = append(checkpointIDs, id)
   935→   935→			}
   936→   936→		}
   937→   937→		return nil
   938→   938→	})
   939→   939→
   940→   940→	return checkpointIDs
   941→   941→}
   942→   942→
   943→   943→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   944→   944→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   945→   945→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   946→   946→	env.T.Helper()
   947→   947→
   948→   948→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   949→   949→	if err != nil {
   950→   950→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   951→   951→	}
   952→   952→
   953→   953→	// Get the branch reference
   954→   954→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   955→   955→	if err != nil {
   956→   956→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   957→   957→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   958→   958→		if err != nil {
   959→   959→			return "", false
   960→   960→		}
   961→   961→	}
   962→   962→
   963→   963→	// Get the commit
   964→   964→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   965→   965→	if err != nil {
   966→   966→		return "", false
   967→   967→	}
   968→   968→
   969→   969→	// Get the tree
   970→   970→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   971→   971→	if err != nil {
   972→   972→		return "", false
   973→   973→	}
   974→   974→
   975→   975→	// Get the file
   976→   976→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   977→   977→	if err != nil {
   978→   978→		return "", false
   979→   979→	}
   980→   980→
   981→   981→	// Get the content
   982→   982→	content, err := file.Contents()
   983→   983→	if err != nil {
   984→   984→		return "", false
   985→   985→	}
   986→   986→
   987→   987→	return content, true
   988→   988→}
   989→   989→
   990→   990→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
   991→   991→type CheckpointValidation struct {
   992→   992→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
   993→   993→	CheckpointID string
   994→   994→
   995→   995→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name (optional)
   996→   996→	Strategy string
   997→   997→
   998→   998→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched (optional)
   999→   999→	FilesTouched []string
  1000→  1000→
  1001→  1001→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt (optional)
  1002→  1002→	ExpectedPrompts []string
  1003→  1003→
  1004→  1004→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl (optional)
  1005→  1005→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
  1006→  1006→}
  1007→  1007→
  1008→  1008→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
  1009→  1009→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
  1010→  1010→	env.T.Helper()
  1011→  1011→
  1012→  1012→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
  1013→  1013→
  1014→  1014→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1015→  1015→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1016→  1016→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1017→  1017→	}
  1018→  1018→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1019→  1019→
  1020→  1020→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1021→  1021→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1022→  1022→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1023→  1023→	if !found {
  1024→  1024→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1025→  1025→	} else {
  1026→  1026→		var summary map[string]any
  1027→  1027→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1028→  1028→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1029→  1029→		} else {
  1030→  1030→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1031→  1031→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1032→  1032→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1033→  1033→			}
  1034→  1034→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1035→  1035→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1036→  1036→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1037→  1037→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1038→  1038→				}
  1039→  1039→			}
  1040→  1040→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1041→  1041→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1042→  1042→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1043→  1043→			}
  1044→  1044→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1045→  1045→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1046→  1046→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1047→  1047→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1048→  1048→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1049→  1049→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1050→  1050→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1051→  1051→						}
  1052→  1052→					}
  1053→  1053→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1054→  1054→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1055→  1055→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1056→  1056→						}
  1057→  1057→					}
  1058→  1058→				}
  1059→  1059→			}
  1060→  1060→		}
  1061→  1061→	}
  1062→  1062→
  1063→  1063→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1064→  1064→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1065→  1065→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1066→  1066→	if !found {
  1067→  1067→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1068→  1068→	} else {
  1069→  1069→		var metadata map[string]any
  1070→  1070→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1071→  1071→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1072→  1072→		} else {
  1073→  1073→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1074→  1074→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1075→  1075→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1076→  1076→			}
  1077→  1077→			// Validate created_at exists
  1078→  1078→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1079→  1079→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1080→  1080→			}
  1081→  1081→		}
  1082→  1082→	}
  1083→  1083→
  1084→  1084→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1085→  1085→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1086→  1086→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1087→  1087→	if !found {
  1088→  1088→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1089→  1089→	} else {
  1090→  1090→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1091→  1091→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1092→  1092→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1093→  1093→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1094→  1094→		validLines := 0
  1095→  1095→		for i, line := range lines {
  1096→  1096→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1097→  1097→			if line == "" {
  1098→  1098→				continue
  1099→  1099→			}
  1100→  1100→			validLines++
  1101→  1101→			var obj map[string]any
  1102→  1102→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1103→  1103→				if isClaudeCode {
  1104→  1104→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1105→  1105→				}
  1106→  1106→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1107→  1107→			}
  1108→  1108→		}
  1109→  1109→		if validLines == 0 {
  1110→  1110→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1111→  1111→		}
  1112→  1112→		// Check expected content
  1113→  1113→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1114→  1114→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1115→  1115→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1116→  1116→			}
  1117→  1117→		}
  1118→  1118→	}
  1119→  1119→
  1120→  1120→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1121→  1121→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1122→  1122→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1123→  1123→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1124→  1124→		if !found {
  1125→  1125→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1126→  1126→		} else {
  1127→  1127→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1128→  1128→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1129→  1129→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1130→  1130→				}
  1131→  1131→			}
  1132→  1132→		}
  1133→  1133→	}
  1134→  1134→
  1135→  1135→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1136→  1136→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1137→  1137→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1138→  1138→	if !found {
  1139→  1139→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1140→  1140→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1141→  1141→		// Verify hash matches
  1142→  1142→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1143→  1143→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1144→  1144→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1145→  1145→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1146→  1146→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1147→  1147→		}
  1148→  1148→	}
  1149→  1149→}
  1150→  1150→

     1→//go:build e2e

     3→package e2e

     5→import (
     6→	"bytes"

    15→
    16→// AgentNameClaudeCode is the name for Claude Code agent.
    17→const AgentNameClaudeCode = "claude-code"
    18→
    19→// AgentNameGemini is the name for Gemini CLI agent.
    20→const AgentNameGemini = "gemini"
    21→
    22→// AgentNameOpenCode is the name for OpenCode agent.
    23→const AgentNameOpenCode = "opencode"
    24→
    25→// AgentRunner abstracts invoking a coding agent for e2e tests.
    26→// This follows the multi-agent pattern from cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go.
    27→type AgentRunner interface {
    28→	// Name returns the agent name (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini-cli")
    29→	Name() string
    30→
    31→	// IsAvailable checks if the agent CLI is installed and authenticated
    32→	IsAvailable() (bool, error)
    33→
    34→	// RunPrompt executes a prompt and returns the result
    35→	RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error)
    36→
    37→	// RunPromptWithTools executes with specific allowed tools
    38→	RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error)
    39→}
    40→
    41→// AgentResult holds the result of an agent invocation.
    42→type AgentResult struct {
    43→	Stdout   string
    44→	Stderr   string
    45→	ExitCode int
    46→	Duration time.Duration
    47→}
    48→
    49→// AgentRunnerConfig holds configuration for agent runners.
    50→type AgentRunnerConfig struct {
    51→	Model   string        // Model to use (e.g., "haiku" for Claude)
    52→	Timeout time.Duration // Timeout per prompt
    53→}
    54→
    55→// NewAgentRunner creates an agent runner based on the agent name.
    56→//
    57→//nolint:ireturn // factory pattern intentionally returns interface
    58→func NewAgentRunner(name string, config AgentRunnerConfig) AgentRunner {
    59→	switch name {
    60→	case AgentNameClaudeCode:
    61→		return NewClaudeCodeRunner(config)
    62→	case AgentNameGemini:
    63→		return NewGeminiCLIRunner(config)
    64→	case AgentNameOpenCode:
    65→		return NewOpenCodeRunner(config)
    66→	default:
    67→		// Return a runner that reports as unavailable
    68→		return &unavailableRunner{name: name}
    69→	}
    70→}
    71→
    72→// unavailableRunner is returned for unknown agent names.
    73→type unavailableRunner struct {
    74→	name string
    75→}
    76→
    77→func (r *unavailableRunner) Name() string { return r.name }
    78→
    79→func (r *unavailableRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
    80→	return false, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s", r.name)
    81→}
    82→
    83→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPrompt(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    84→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    85→}
    86→
    87→func (r *unavailableRunner) RunPromptWithTools(_ context.Context, _ string, _ string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
    88→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s is not available", r.name)
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ClaudeCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for Claude Code CLI.
    92→type ClaudeCodeRunner struct {
    93→	Model        string
    94→	Timeout      time.Duration
    95→	AllowedTools []string
    96→}
    97→
    98→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    99→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {
   100→	model := config.Model
   101→	if model == "" {
   102→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_CLAUDE_MODEL")
   103→		if model == "" {
   104→			model = "haiku"
   105→		}
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	timeout := config.Timeout
   109→	if timeout == 0 {
   110→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   111→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   112→				timeout = parsed
   113→			}
   114→		}
   115→		if timeout == 0 {
   116→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   117→		}
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	return &ClaudeCodeRunner{
   121→		Model:        model,
   122→		Timeout:      timeout,
   123→		AllowedTools: []string{"Edit", "Read", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"},
   124→	}
   125→}
   126→
   127→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) Name() string {
   128→	return AgentNameClaudeCode
   129→}
   130→
   131→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   132→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   133→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   134→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   135→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   136→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   137→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   138→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   142→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   143→	defer cancel()
   144→
   145→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   146→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   147→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return true, nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   154→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   155→}
   156→
   157→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   158→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   159→	args := []string{
   160→		"--model", r.Model,
   161→		"-p", prompt,
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   165→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   166→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   167→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   168→		args = append(args, tools...)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Create context with timeout
   172→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   173→	defer cancel()
   174→
   175→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   176→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   177→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   178→
   179→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   180→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   181→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   182→
   183→	start := time.Now()
   184→	err := cmd.Run()
   185→	duration := time.Since(start)
   186→
   187→	result := &AgentResult{
   188→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   189→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   190→		Duration: duration,
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   195→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   196→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   197→		} else {
   198→			result.ExitCode = -1
   199→		}
   200→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   201→		return result, err
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	result.ExitCode = 0
   205→	return result, nil
   206→}
   207→
   208→// GeminiCLIRunner implements AgentRunner for Gemini CLI.
   209→// See: https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli
   210→type GeminiCLIRunner struct {
   211→	Model   string
   212→	Timeout time.Duration
   213→}
   214→
   215→// NewGeminiCLIRunner creates a new Gemini CLI runner with the given config.
   216→func NewGeminiCLIRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *GeminiCLIRunner {
   217→	model := config.Model
   218→	if model == "" {
   219→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_GEMINI_MODEL")
   220→		// Default to gemini-2.5-flash if not specified
   221→		if model == "" {
   222→			model = "gemini-2.5-flash"
   223→		}
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	timeout := config.Timeout
   227→	if timeout == 0 {
   228→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   229→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   230→				timeout = parsed
   231→			}
   232→		}
   233→		if timeout == 0 {
   234→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   235→		}
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	return &GeminiCLIRunner{
   239→		Model:   model,
   240→		Timeout: timeout,
   241→	}
   242→}
   243→
   244→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) Name() string {
   245→	return AgentNameGemini
   246→}
   247→
   248→// IsAvailable checks if Gemini CLI is installed and authenticated.
   249→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   250→	// Check if gemini CLI is in PATH
   251→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("gemini"); err != nil {
   252→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Check if gemini is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   256→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   257→	defer cancel()
   258→
   259→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", "--version")
   260→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   261→		return false, fmt.Errorf("gemini CLI not working: %w", err)
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	return true, nil
   265→}
   266→
   267→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   268→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   269→}
   270→
   271→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   272→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   273→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   274→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   275→	args := []string{
   276→		"-m", r.Model,
   277→		"-p", prompt,
   278→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   282→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   283→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   284→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   285→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   286→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   287→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   291→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   292→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   293→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create context with timeout
   297→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   298→	defer cancel()
   299→
   300→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   301→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   302→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   303→
   304→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   305→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   306→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   307→
   308→	start := time.Now()
   309→	err := cmd.Run()
   310→	duration := time.Since(start)
   311→
   312→	result := &AgentResult{
   313→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   314→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   315→		Duration: duration,
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   320→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   321→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   322→		} else {
   323→			result.ExitCode = -1
   324→		}
   325→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   326→		return result, err
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	result.ExitCode = 0
   330→	return result, nil
   331→}
   332→
   333→// OpenCodeRunner implements AgentRunner for OpenCode.
   334→// See: https://opencode.ai/docs/cli/
   335→type OpenCodeRunner struct {
   336→	Model   string
   337→	Timeout time.Duration
   338→}
   339→
   340→// NewOpenCodeRunner creates a new OpenCode runner with the given config.
   341→func NewOpenCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *OpenCodeRunner {
   342→	model := config.Model
   343→	if model == "" {
   344→		model = os.Getenv("E2E_OPENCODE_MODEL")
   345→		// No default model - OpenCode uses whatever is configured in the project
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	timeout := config.Timeout
   349→	if timeout == 0 {
   350→		if envTimeout := os.Getenv("E2E_TIMEOUT"); envTimeout != "" {
   351→			if parsed, err := time.ParseDuration(envTimeout); err == nil {
   352→				timeout = parsed
   353→			}
   354→		}
   355→		if timeout == 0 {
   356→			timeout = 2 * time.Minute
   357→		}
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	return &OpenCodeRunner{
   361→		Model:   model,
   362→		Timeout: timeout,
   363→	}
   364→}
   365→
   366→func (r *OpenCodeRunner) Name() string {
   367→	return AgentNameOpenCode
   368→}
   369→
   370→// IsAvailable checks if OpenCode CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   371→func (r *OpenCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   372→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("opencode"); err != nil {
   373→		return false, fmt.Errorf("opencode CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   377→	defer cancel()
   378→
   379→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "--version")
   380→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   381→		return false, fmt.Errorf("opencode CLI not working: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	return true, nil
   385→}
   386→
   387→func (r *OpenCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   388→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, nil)
   389→}
   390→
   391→func (r *OpenCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, _ []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   392→	// Build command: opencode run "<prompt>"
   393→	// In non-interactive mode, all permissions are auto-approved.
   394→	args := []string{"run"}
   395→
   396→	if r.Model != "" {
   397→		args = append(args, "--model", r.Model)
   398→	}
   399→
   400→	args = append(args, prompt)
   401→
   402→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   403→	defer cancel()
   404→
   405→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", args...)
   406→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   407→
   408→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   409→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   410→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   411→
   412→	start := time.Now()
   413→	err := cmd.Run()
   414→	duration := time.Since(start)
   415→
   416→	result := &AgentResult{
   417→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   418→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   419→		Duration: duration,
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	if err != nil {
   423→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   424→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   425→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   426→		} else {
   427→			result.ExitCode = -1
   428→		}
   429→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   430→		return result, err
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	result.ExitCode = 0
   434→	return result, nil
   435→}
   436→

> AGENT

I found the issue. For OpenCode, the transcript file (`.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`) is only created at `turn-end` when `fetchAndCacheExport` is called. But when the agent makes commits mid-turn, `post-commit` tries to read this file for condensation and it doesn't exist yet.

The fix is to export the transcript at `turn-start` as well, not just `turn-end`. Let me implement this:

> TOOL

tool_use Read
id: toolu_01E8WRyuMpeHjektLkqGGZUr
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli_worktree1/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go"
}
```

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"io"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"path/filepath"
     9→	"time"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// Hook name constants — these become CLI subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`.
    16→const (
    17→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
    18→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
    19→	HookNameTurnStart    = "turn-start"
    20→	HookNameTurnEnd      = "turn-end"
    21→	HookNameCompaction   = "compaction"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    25→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    26→	return []string{
    27→		HookNameSessionStart,
    28→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    29→		HookNameTurnStart,
    30→		HookNameTurnEnd,
    31→		HookNameCompaction,
    32→	}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    36→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    37→	switch hookName {
    38→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    39→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    40→		if err != nil {
    41→			return nil, err
    42→		}
    43→		return &agent.Event{
    44→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    45→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    46→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    47→		}, nil
    48→
    49→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    50→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    51→		if err != nil {
    52→			return nil, err
    53→		}
    54→		// Get the temp file path for this session (may not exist yet, but needed for pre-prompt state).
    55→		repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    56→		if err != nil {
    57→			repoRoot = "."
    58→		}
    59→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    60→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    61→		return &agent.Event{
    62→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    63→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    64→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    65→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    66→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    67→		}, nil
    68→
    69→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    70→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    71→		if err != nil {
    72→			return nil, err
    73→		}
    74→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    75→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    76→		if exportErr != nil {
    77→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    78→		}
    79→		return &agent.Event{
    80→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    81→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    82→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    83→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    84→		}, nil
    85→
    86→	case HookNameCompaction:
    87→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			return nil, err
    90→		}
    91→		return &agent.Event{
    92→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    93→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    94→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    95→		}, nil
    96→
    97→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    98→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    99→		if err != nil {
   100→			return nil, err
   101→		}
   102→		return &agent.Event{
   103→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   104→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   105→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   106→		}, nil
   107→
   108→	default:
   109→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   110→	}
   111→}
   112→
   113→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   114→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   115→//
   116→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   117→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   118→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   119→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   120→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   121→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   122→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		repoRoot = "."
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   128→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   129→
   130→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   131→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   132→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   133→			return tmpFile, nil
   134→		}
   135→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   139→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Validate output is valid JSON before caching
   145→	if !json.Valid(data) {
   146→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export returned invalid JSON (%d bytes)", len(data))
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   150→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   151→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   155→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	return tmpFile, nil
   159→}
   160→

> TOOL

> AGENT

All tests pass. The fix I made ensures that when OpenCode fires the `turn-start` hook, it exports the transcript immediately to `.entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json`. This way, when the agent makes commits mid-turn (before `turn-end`), the transcript file exists and condensation can read it for metadata storage.

The change was in `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go`:
- Modified the `turn-start` case to call `fetchAndCacheExport` to pre-populate the transcript file
- Added graceful fallback if the export fails (uses computed path, and condensation may fail later)

The user can now run the E2E test `TestE2E_Scenario3_MultipleGranularCommits` with OpenCode and it should pass, as the transcript will be available for mid-turn commit condensation.

> DEVELOPER

hmm, but then the script is missing the steps until the commit?

> AGENT

You're right. Exporting at `turn-start` only captures the transcript state *before* the agent has done any work. The agent's actions (creating files, running git commands) happen *after* `turn-start`, so the exported transcript won't include them.

The transcript needs to be refreshed right before condensation reads it. Let me implement a proper fix:

1. Have OpenCode implement `TranscriptPreparer` interface
2. Call `PrepareTranscript` in the condensation code path before reading the transcript

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"

    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"

    14→
    15→// Hook name constants — these become CLI subcommands under `entire hooks opencode`.
    16→const (
    17→	HookNameSessionStart = "session-start"
    18→	HookNameSessionEnd   = "session-end"
    19→	HookNameTurnStart    = "turn-start"
    20→	HookNameTurnEnd      = "turn-end"
    21→	HookNameCompaction   = "compaction"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    25→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    26→	return []string{
    27→		HookNameSessionStart,
    28→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    29→		HookNameTurnStart,
    30→		HookNameTurnEnd,
    31→		HookNameCompaction,
    32→	}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// ParseHookEvent translates OpenCode hook calls into normalized lifecycle events.
    36→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    37→	switch hookName {
    38→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    39→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    40→		if err != nil {
    41→			return nil, err
    42→		}
    43→		return &agent.Event{
    44→			Type:      agent.SessionStart,
    45→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    46→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    47→		}, nil
    48→
    49→	case HookNameTurnStart:
    50→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[turnStartRaw](stdin)
    51→		if err != nil {
    52→			return nil, err
    53→		}
    54→		// Get the temp file path for this session.
    55→		// The file may not exist yet - it will be created when PrepareTranscript
    56→		// is called (before reading) or at turn-end via fetchAndCacheExport.
    57→		repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    58→		if err != nil {
    59→			repoRoot = "."
    60→		}
    61→		tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
    62→		transcriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, raw.SessionID+".json")
    63→		return &agent.Event{
    64→			Type:       agent.TurnStart,
    65→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    66→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    67→			Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
    68→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    69→		}, nil
    70→
    71→	case HookNameTurnEnd:
    72→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    73→		if err != nil {
    74→			return nil, err
    75→		}
    76→		// Call `opencode export` to get the transcript and write to temp file
    77→		transcriptPath, exportErr := a.fetchAndCacheExport(raw.SessionID)
    78→		if exportErr != nil {
    79→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export session: %w", exportErr)
    80→		}
    81→		return &agent.Event{
    82→			Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
    83→			SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
    84→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
    85→			Timestamp:  time.Now(),
    86→		}, nil
    87→
    88→	case HookNameCompaction:
    89→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    90→		if err != nil {
    91→			return nil, err
    92→		}
    93→		return &agent.Event{
    94→			Type:      agent.Compaction,
    95→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
    96→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		}, nil
    98→
    99→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
   100→		raw, err := agent.ReadAndParseHookInput[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   101→		if err != nil {
   102→			return nil, err
   103→		}
   104→		return &agent.Event{
   105→			Type:      agent.SessionEnd,
   106→			SessionID: raw.SessionID,
   107→			Timestamp: time.Now(),
   108→		}, nil
   109→
   110→	default:
   111→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action for unknown hooks
   112→	}
   113→}
   114→
   115→// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result
   116→// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.
   117→//
   118→// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the
   119→// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must
   120→// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before
   121→// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.
   122→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   123→	// Get repo root for the temp directory
   124→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		repoRoot = "."
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	tmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)
   130→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+".json")
   131→
   132→	// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export
   133→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT") != "" {
   134→		if _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {
   135→			return tmpFile, nil
   136→		}
   137→		return "", fmt.Errorf("mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)", tmpFile)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)
   141→	data, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export failed: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// Validate output is valid JSON before caching
   147→	if !json.Valid(data) {
   148→		return "", fmt.Errorf("opencode export returned invalid JSON (%d bytes)", len(data))
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	// Write to temp directory under .entire
   152→	if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   153→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp dir: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   157→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write export file: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	return tmpFile, nil
   161→}
   162→

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→	AgentNameOpenCode   AgentName = "opencode"
    97→)
    98→
    99→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
   100→const (
   101→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   102→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   103→	AgentTypeOpenCode   AgentType = "OpenCode"
   104→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   105→)
   106→
   107→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   108→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   109→
   110→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   111→//
   112→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   113→// This is acceptable because:
   114→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   115→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   116→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   117→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   118→//
   119→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   120→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   121→	registryMu.RLock()
   122→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   123→
   124→	for _, factory := range registry {
   125→		ag := factory()
   126→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   127→			return ag, nil
   128→		}
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   132→}
   133→
   134→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   135→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   136→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   137→	registryMu.RLock()
   138→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   139→	for _, f := range registry {
   140→		factories = append(factories, f)
   141→	}
   142→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   143→
   144→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   145→	var dirs []string
   146→	for _, factory := range factories {
   147→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   148→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   149→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   150→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   151→			}
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   155→	return dirs
   156→}
   157→
   158→// Default returns the default agent.
   159→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   160→//
   161→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   162→func Default() Agent {
   163→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   164→	return a
   165→}
   166→

   447→
   448→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   449→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   450→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   451→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   452→
   453→	// Read the live transcript
   454→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   455→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   464→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   465→	}
   466→
   467→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   468→	data.Transcript = liveData
   469→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   470→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   471→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   472→
   473→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   474→	// (no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   475→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   476→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   477→	} else {
   478→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   479→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   480→	}
   481→
   482→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   483→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   484→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	return data, nil
   488→}
   489→
   490→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   491→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   492→// For Gemini CLI, OpenCode, and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   493→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   494→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   495→	if content == "" {
   496→		return 0
   497→	}
   498→
   499→	// OpenCode uses export JSON format with {"info": {...}, "messages": [...]}
   500→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   501→		session, err := opencode.ParseExportSession([]byte(content))
   502→		if err == nil && session != nil {
   503→			return len(session.Messages)
   504→		}

  "new_string": "// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.\n// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {\n\tdata := &ExtractedSessionData{}\n\n\t// Read the live transcript\n\tif state.TranscriptPath == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"no transcript path in session state\")\n\t}\n\n\t// If the agent implements TranscriptPreparer, call PrepareTranscript to ensure\n\t// the transcript file is up-to-date. This is needed for OpenCode which exports\n\t// the transcript on-demand rather than writing it continuously.\n\tif state.AgentType != \"\" {\n\t\tif ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType); err == nil {\n\t\t\tif preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {\n\t\t\t\tif err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(state.TranscriptPath); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t// Log but continue - the file might already exist from a previous export\n\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Debug(context.Background(), \"failed to prepare transcript\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"error\", err, \"path\", state.TranscriptPath)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tliveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)"

> AGENT

All tests pass. Let me summarize the fix:

**The Problem:**
When OpenCode makes commits mid-turn (before `turn-end`), the `post-commit` hook tries to read the transcript file for condensation. But the transcript file only gets created when `opencode export` is called, which previously only happened at `turn-end`. So the transcript was either missing or stale.

**The Fix:**
1. Added `PrepareTranscript` method to OpenCodeAgent implementing the `TranscriptPreparer` interface - this calls `opencode export` to refresh the transcript file

2. Modified `extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript` in the condensation code to call `PrepareTranscript` before reading the transcript file

Now when the `post-commit` hook runs for mid-turn commits:
1. It calls `extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript`
2. Which calls `PrepareTranscript` for OpenCode
3. Which calls `opencode export` to get the latest transcript with all the agent's actions
4. Then reads and processes the refreshed transcript

This ensures the transcript contains all agent actions up to the commit point, not just from the start of the turn.
